		private static ImageList m_BitmapImages;

		private static SortedList myApedDatabases = null;

		public override ContextMenu ContextMenu
		{
			get
			{
				return ((Control)this).ContextMenu;
			}
			set
			{
				((Control)this).ContextMenu = value;
			}
		}

		public ApedDataManagerSnapIn()
		{
			if (myNodeDatabases != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				AddAllImagesToSnapIn();
				AddAllBitmapsToList();
				SetNodeIcons();
				InitToolBars();
				AppDomain currentDomain = AppDomain.CurrentDomain;
				currentDomain.AssemblyLoad += objDomain_AssemblyLoad;
				currentDomain.AssemblyResolve += objDomain_AssemblyResolve;
				currentDomain.ProcessExit += objDomain_ProcessExit;
				GlobalControl.Parent = (Control)(object)this;
				((Control)this).Click += ApedDataManagerSnapIn_Click;
				myNodeDatabases = new DatabasesNode((SnapinBase)(object)this, out myApedDatabases, out myNewDatabases);
				((Component)this).Disposed += ApedDataManagerSnapIn_Disposed;
				((Control)this).VisibleChanged += ApedDataManagerSnapIn_VisibleChanged;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				error = ex.Message;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (myNodeDatabases.bSLMCheck)
				{
					myNodeDatabases = null;
					myApedDatabases = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ApedDataManagerSnapIn_VisibleChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void ApedDataManagerSnapIn_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			GC.Collect();
		}

		public override void Destroy()
		{
			ApedApplication.APEDHtmlHelpClose();
			if (myNodeDatabases != null)
			{
				myNodeDatabases.ReleaseLicense();
			}
			if (myApedDatabases != null && myApedDatabases.Count > 0)
			{
				ApedSharedMethods.CleanDatabasesNode(myApedDatabases);
			}
			if (myNewDatabases != null)
			{
				_ = myNewDatabases.Count;
				_ = 0;
			}
			Process[] processes = Process.GetProcesses();
			foreach (Process process in processes)
			{
				if (process.ProcessName.StartsWith("mmc"))
				{
					process.Kill();
					process.WaitForExit();
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddAllImagesToSnapIn()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < IconFilePaths.Length; i++)
			{
				((SnapinBase)this).AddImage(IconFilePaths[i]);
			}
		}

		private void AddAllBitmapsToList()
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			m_BitmapImages = new ImageList();
			for (int i = 0; i < BitmapFilePaths.Length; i++)
			{
				m_BitmapImages.Add(BitmapFilePaths[i], Color.Black);
			}
		}

		private void SetNodeIcons()
		{
			BaseNode.DefaultResultPaneImages = ((SnapinBase)this).Images;
		}

		private void InitToolBars()
		{
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (base.m_Component == null)
			{
				base.m_Component = new Component((SnapinBase)(object)this);
			}
			DatabasesNode.InitToolBar(this);
			DatabaseNode.InitToolBar(this);
			CompoundsNode.InitToolBar(this);
			PropertiesAndParametersNode.InitToolBar(this);
			DatabanksNode.InitToolBar(this);
			PureNode.InitToolBar(this);
			BinaryNode.InitToolBar(this);
			PairNode.InitToolBar(this);
			TernaryNode.InitToolBar(this);
			CompoundNode.InitToolBar(this);
			DatabankNode.InitToolBar(this);
			PureDataNode.InitToolBar(this);
			BinaryDataNode.InitToolBar(this);
			PairDataNode.InitToolBar(this);
			ReactionsNode.InitToolBar(this);
		}

		private void objDomain_ProcessExit(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}

		{
		}

		private Assembly objDomain_AssemblyResolve(object sender, ResolveEventArgs args)
		{
			return null;
		}

		private void ApedDataManagerSnapIn_Disposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).Dispose(disposing: true);
			((SnapinBase)this).Destroy();
			((Component)(object)this).Dispose(disposing: true);
			((SnapinBase)this).Destroy();
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).OnKeyDown(e);
		}

		protected override void OnClick(EventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).OnClick(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyPress(KeyPressEventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).OnKeyPress(e);
		}

		protected override void OnHelpRequested(HelpEventArgs hevent)
		{
			((Control)this).OnHelpRequested(hevent);
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			((Control)this).OnContextMenuChanged(e);
		}

		public int GetLinkedTopics(out IntPtr lpCompiledHelpFiles)
		{
			lpCompiledHelpFiles = IntPtr.Zero;
			return 1;
		}

		public int GetHelpTopic(out IntPtr lpCompiledHelpFile)
		{
			lpCompiledHelpFile = Marshal.StringToCoTaskMemUni(GlobalControl.HelpFile);
			return 0;
		}

	public class ApedSQLAuthPreferenceForm : Form
	{
		private const string SQL_AUTHENTICATION_REGKEY = "SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APED\\41.0";

		private const string SQL_AUTHENTICATION_REGVAL = "UserAuthenticationMethod";

		private int localMode = -1;

		private IContainer components;

		private RadioButton radioButton1;

		private RadioButton radioButton2;

		private Button button1;

		private Button button2;

		private Label label1;

		private GroupBox groupBox1;

		private Label label2;

		public ApedSQLAuthPreferenceForm()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			if (ApedApplication.GetSQLAuthPreference() == 2)
			{
				radioButton1.Checked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				radioButton2.Checked = true;
			}
			_ = GlobalControl.localDBServer;
		}

		private void ChangeSQLAuthPreference()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APED\\41.0", writable: true);
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APED\\41.0");
				}
				if (registryKey == null)
				{
					throw new GatewayException("An error occurred setting the SQL Authentication registry key.");
				}
				if (radioButton1.Checked)
				{
					registryKey.SetValue("UserAuthenticationMethod", 2);
					GlobalControl.authenticationMode = 2;
				}
				else if (radioButton2.Checked)
				{
					registryKey.SetValue("UserAuthenticationMethod", 1);
					GlobalControl.authenticationMode = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception inner)
			{
				throw new GatewayException("An error occurred setting the configuration file path [2]", inner);
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ChangeSQLAuthPreference();
			Close();
		}

		private void button2_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private void radioButton1_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			_ = radioButton1.Checked;
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && components != null)
			{
				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

	internal class BinaryDataNode : FormNode, IFindCompoundLauncher
	{
		private IDatabase myDatabase;

		private IDatabank myDatabank;

		private IDatabankCollection myDatabanks;

		private DbankCompoundList myRootCompounds;

		private DbankCompoundList mySelectedCompounds;

		private ICompoundPropertyGateway myCompGateway;

		private IApedDatabase myApedDatabase;

		private IApedDatabank myApedDatabank;

		private bool mybShow;

		private ApedNodeType myNodeType = (ApedNodeType)14;

		private string myNodeName = "";

		private IApedMMC myDataManager;

		public bool bShow
		{
			get
			{
				return mybShow;
			}
			set
			{
				mybShow = value;
			}
		}

		public IDatabase Database => myDatabase;

		public BinaryDataNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, DbankCompoundList selectedCompounds, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, IApedDatabank apedDatabank, IApedMMC dataManager)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		private bool DBContainCompound(IDbankCompound dbc, ArrayList dbankcompIDs)
		{
			foreach (int dbankcompID in dbankcompIDs)
			{
				string text = dbankcompID.ToString();
				if (text == dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected internal void LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			DbankCompoundDialogBox val = new DbankCompoundDialogBox((IFindCompoundLauncher)(object)this);
			if ((int)val.TabDialogControl.OKorCancel == 1)
			{
				if (val != null)
				{
					val = null;
				}
			}
			else if (val != null)
			{
				val = null;
			}
		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedBinaryDataTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					val.ReleaseExcel();
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowCompounds();
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing binary properties: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a0: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowCompounds();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 22)
			{
				_ = database == myApedDatabase.Name;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0075: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0077: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedBinaryDataTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowAllCompounds(val);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedBinaryDataTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedBinaryDataTabCtl binaryDataTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				binaryDataTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				binaryDataTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		}

			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabank != null)
			{
				selectedDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			}
			else
			{
				selectedDbanks = null;
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedDatabanks(IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
		}

		public void GetAvailableDatabanks(out IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			IDatabank databank = myApedDatabase.Databank;
			availableDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { databank });
		}

		public void SetAvailableDatabanks(IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
		}

		{
			return true;
		}

		{

		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName) || !ApedGlobal.bEdit)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedBinaryTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if (ApedGlobal.bEdit)
					{
						ApedGlobal.bEdit = false;
						val.CheckAnyPendingChanges();
					}
					else
					{
						val.ReleaseExcel();
					}
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedBinaryTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 17 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown

				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedBinaryTabCtl val = (ApedBinaryTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if ((int)updateType == 2)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					else
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown

				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedBinaryTabCtl binaryTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.bDatabanks)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllDatabanks(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				binaryTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				binaryTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)

			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

	public class BinaryParameterEditForm : Form
	{
		private Container components;

		private Rectangle rectOrigFormBounds_;

		private Rectangle rectOrigControlBounds_;

		public BinaryParameterEditForm()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			SaveOrigBounds();
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && components != null)
			{
				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void InitializeComponent()
		{
			System.ComponentModel.ComponentResourceManager resources = new System.ComponentModel.ComponentResourceManager(typeof(AspenTech.APED.ApedDataManager.BinaryParameterEditForm));
			base.SuspendLayout();
			this.AutoScaleBaseSize = new System.Drawing.Size(6, 15);
			base.ClientSize = new System.Drawing.Size(560, 488);
			base.Icon = (System.Drawing.Icon)resources.GetObject("$this.Icon");
			base.Name = "BinaryParameterEditForm";
			base.StartPosition = System.Windows.Forms.FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
			this.Text = "Edit Binary Interaction Parameters";
			base.Closing += new System.ComponentModel.CancelEventHandler(OnClosing);
			base.Resize += new System.EventHandler(OnResize);
			base.ResumeLayout(false);
		}

		private void OnClosing(object sender, CancelEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void SaveOrigBounds()
		{
			rectOrigFormBounds_ = base.ClientRectangle;
		}

		private void OnResize(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Rectangle clientRectangle = ((Control)sender).ClientRectangle;
			if (clientRectangle.Right >= rectOrigFormBounds_.Right || clientRectangle.Bottom >= rectOrigFormBounds_.Bottom)
			{
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = 0;
				if (clientRectangle.Right > rectOrigFormBounds_.Right)
				{
					num = clientRectangle.Right - rectOrigFormBounds_.Right;
				}
				if (clientRectangle.Bottom > rectOrigFormBounds_.Bottom)
				{
					num2 = clientRectangle.Bottom - rectOrigFormBounds_.Bottom;
				}
			}
		}
	}

		public CompoundNode(SnapinBase snapin, CompoundsNode parentNode, string compoundName, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList selectedCompounds, IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, SortedList apedDatabases, IApedMMC dataManager)

			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				if (myCompoundName == val.AspenName)
				{
					myDBCompound = val;
				}
			}
			myCompGateway = (ICompoundPropertyGateway)new CompoundPropertyGateway();
			myCompGateway.Databanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			if (myDatabase != null && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				mybLocalDB = true;
			}
			myNode = this;
			myNodeName = myApedDatabase.Name + compoundName;
			Initialize((BaseNode)(object)parentNode, compoundName);
			SetCompoundNodeResultPaneImages(snapin);
		}

		private void Initialize(BaseNode parentNode, string compoundName)
		{
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = compoundName;
			SetImageIndicesForNode();
			AddHandlers();
			if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Pure)
			{
				AddMenuItems();
				((FormNode)this).ControlType = typeof(ApedPureCompoundTabCtl);
			}
			else if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Global)
			{
				myApedNode = (IApedNode)new ApedNode();
				myApedNode.Name = compoundName;
				SortedList sortedList;
				if (myApedDatabase.ApedNodes == null)
				{
					sortedList = new SortedList();
					sortedList.Add(myApedNode.Name, myApedNode);
				}
				else
				{
					sortedList = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.ApedNodes;
					if (!sortedList.Contains(myApedNode.Name))
					{
						sortedList.Add(myApedNode.Name, myApedNode);
					}
				}
				myApedDatabase.ApedNodes = sortedList;
				AddGlobalMenuItems();
				((FormNode)this).ControlType = typeof(ApedCompoundTabCtl);
			}
			else if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Interaction)
			{
				AddGlobalMenuItems();
				((FormNode)this).ControlType = typeof(ApedPureCompoundTabCtl);
			}
		}

		private void Initialize(BaseNode parentNode)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0083: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string compoundIdentifierType = myNodeDatabase.UserOptions.CompoundIdentifierType;
			string text = ((CompoundObject)myCompound).Compound1Name;
			if (text == null || text == "")
			{
				text = myCompound.CompoundIndex.ToString();
			}
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = text;
			SetImageIndicesForNode();
			AddHandlers();
			if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Pure)
			{
				AddMenuItems();
				((FormNode)this).ControlType = typeof(ApedPureCompoundTabCtl);
			}
			else if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Global)
			{

				myApedNode.Name = text;
				SortedList sortedList;
				if (myApedDatabase.ApedNodes == null)
				{

					sortedList.Add(myApedNode.Name, myApedNode);

			else if (myCompoundNodeType == CompoundNodeType.Interaction)

		}

		private void AddHandlers()
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected O, but got Unknown

		private void AddGlobalMenuItems()
		{
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (GlobalControl.localDBServer || GlobalControl.runAdmin)
			{
				MenuCommandHandler val = new MenuCommandHandler(OnDeleteThisCompound);
				MenuItem val2 = new MenuItem("&Delete...", "Permanently delete this compound from the database", val);
				((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);
			}
		}

		private void OnDeleteThisCompound(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to permamnently delete Compound " + myCompoundName + " in Database " + myDatabase.Alias + "? ", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				ICompoundManager val = null;
				IDatabase obj2 = myDatabase;
				ISupportsCompounds val2 = (ISupportsCompounds)(object)((obj2 is ISupportsCompounds) ? obj2 : null);
				if (val2 != null)
				{
					val = val2.CompoundManager;
				}
				if (val != null)
				{
					ICompound compound = myDBCompound.Compound;
					if (compound != null)
					{
						bool flag = true;
						try
						{
							val.RemoveCompound(compound);
						}
						catch (GatewayException ex)
						{
							if (ex.Message == "")
							{
								string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
								if (text != "")
								{
									MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							}
						}
						catch (Exception ex2)
						{
							if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object") && !ex2.Message.Contains("AxHost"))
							{
								MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							}
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
						}
					}
				}
				RemoveThisNode();
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		protected internal void RemoveThisNode()
		{
			((CompoundsNode)(object)((BaseNode)this).ParentNode).RemoveCompound(this);
			((BaseNode)this).ParentNode.Children.Remove(this);
		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName) || !ApedGlobal.bEdit)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedCompoundTabCtl val = (ApedCompoundTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					ApedGlobal.bEdit = false;
					val.CheckAnyPendingChanges();
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object") && !ex2.Message.Contains("AxHost"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{

				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);

			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00c5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c8: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedCompoundTabCtl val = (ApedCompoundTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowCompoundGlobalProperties(val);
						((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object") && !ex2.Message.Contains("AxHost"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 15)
			{
				_ = database == myApedDatabase.Name;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedCompoundTabCtl val = (ApedCompoundTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if ((int)updateType == 2)
					{
						ShowCompoundGlobalProperties(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					else
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object") && !ex2.Message.Contains("AxHost"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompoundGlobalProperties(ApedCompoundTabCtl compoundTabCtl)
		{
			if (myApedDatabase.GlobalProperties == null || myApedDatabase.bGlobals)
			{
				myApedDatabase.GlobalProperties = null;
				myApedDatabase.PropertiesAndParameters = null;
				ApedUtils.GetAllIdentifiers(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				ApedUtils.GetAllProperties(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				myApedDatabase.bGlobals = false;
				myApedDatabase.bProperties = false;
			}
			ApedSharedMethods.CleanCompoundNode(myApedDatabase);
			compoundTabCtl.PopulateGlobalPropertiesFromCompound(myDBCompound);
			if (myDBCompound.AspenName == myApedNode.Name)
			{
				myApedNode.bGlobalCompound = true;
				myApedNode.CompoundTabCtl = compoundTabCtl;
				SortedList sortedList = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.ApedNodes;
				string aspenName = myDBCompound.AspenName;
				int index = sortedList.IndexOfKey(aspenName);
				sortedList.SetByIndex(index, myApedNode);
				myApedDatabase.ApedNodes = sortedList;
			}
		}

		}

		}

		public CompoundNode(SnapinBase snapin, CompoundsNode parentNode, ICompoundObject compound, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList selectedCompounds, IDatabase database, SortedList apedDatabases, IApedDatabank apedDatabank)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ec: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00f5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ff: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_011e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			for (int i = 0; i < myApedDatabases.Count; i++)
			{
				IApedDatabase val = (IApedDatabase)myApedDatabases.GetByIndex(i);
				if (val.Name == myDatabase.Alias)
				{
					myApedDatabase = val;
					myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
					myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
				}
			}
			myCompound = compound;
			myCompoundNodeType = CompoundNodeType.Global;
			myNodeDatabase = (DatabaseNode)(object)((BaseNode)parentNode).ParentNode;
			myNodeDatabank = null;
			myDatabank = databank;

			myDatabanks = (IDatabankCollection)myApedDatabase.Databanks;
			mySelectedCompounds = selectedCompounds;
			myCompGateway = (ICompoundPropertyGateway)new CompoundPropertyGateway();
			myCompGateway.Databanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			Initialize((BaseNode)(object)parentNode);
			SetCompoundNodeResultPaneImages(snapin);
		}

			{

			{

				if (val.Name == "&Delete...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private IApedMMC myDataManager;

		public CompoundsNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, SortedList apedDatabases, IApedMMC dataManager)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
			myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			myDataManager = dataManager;
			if (myCompoundManager == null)
			{
				IDatabase obj = myDatabase;
				ISupportsCompounds val = (ISupportsCompounds)(object)((obj is ISupportsCompounds) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					myCompoundManager = val.CompoundManager;
					if (myIdentifier == null)
					{
						myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref myIdentifier);
					}
				}
			}
			if (myDatabase != null && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				mybLocalDB = true;
			}
			myNode = this;
			myNodeName = myApedDatabase.Name + "Selected Compounds";
			Initialize();
			SetCompoundsNodeResultPaneImages(snapin);
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = "Selected Compounds";

			AddHandlers();
			AddMenuItems();

		}

		private void AddHandlers()

			myCompounds.CompoundsAdded += AddCompoundsToSelectedList;
			myCompounds.CompoundsRemoved += RemoveCompoundsFromSelectedList;
			myCompounds.DatabaseUpdated += OnDatabaseUpdated;
			myApedDatabase.DatabaseUpdate += new ApedDatabaseEventHandler(OnUpdateDatabase);
			myCompounds.CompoundAdded += AddCompoundToSelectedList;
			myCompounds.CompoundRemoved += RemoveCompoundFromSelectedList;
			myDataManager.DataManagerUpdate += new ApedMMCEventHandler(OnDataManagerUpdate);
		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedSelectedCompoundsTabCtl val = (ApedSelectedCompoundsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					val.ReleaseExcel();
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		}

		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			mybExpand = true;
			try
			{
				updateChildNodes((ApedNodeType)(-1));
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode2 = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode2).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowCompounds();
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing Compound Table: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00e6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e9: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_026b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_026d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_01d4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0129: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList changedCompoundList = null;
			ArrayList newCompoundList = null;
			if (database == myApedDatabase.Name && ApedGlobal.DataManager.ChangedCompounds != null)
			{
				GetChangedCompoundList(out changedCompoundList);
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds)
			{
				GetNewCompoundList(out newCompoundList);
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowCompounds();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 10 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				if (changedCompoundList != null && changedCompoundList.Count > 0)
				{
					try
					{
						myCompounds.Clear();
						myCompounds.Adds(changedCompoundList, myNodeType, myNodeName);
					}
					catch (GatewayException ex3)
					{
						if (ex3.Message == "")
						{
							string text2 = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
							if (text2 != "")
							{
								MessageBox.Show(text2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							MessageBox.Show(ex3.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception ex4)
					{
						if (!ex4.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
						{
							MessageBox.Show(ex4.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
				}
				if (newCompoundList == null || newCompoundList.Count <= 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					myCompounds.Adds(newCompoundList, myNodeType, myNodeName);
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex5)
				{
					if (ex5.Message == "")
					{
						string text3 = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text3 != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text3, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex5.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex6)
				{
					if (!ex6.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex6.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
				IDatabase obj = myDatabase;
				ISupportsCompounds val = (ISupportsCompounds)(object)((obj is ISupportsCompounds) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					myCompoundManager = val.CompoundManager;
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref myIdentifier);
				}
			}
		}

		private void GetChangedCompoundList(out ArrayList changedCompoundList)
		{
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0094: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_019c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01a3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			changedCompoundList = null;
			if (myCompounds.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			SortedList sortedList = new SortedList();
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				sortedList.Add(val.AspenName, val);
			}
			bool flag = false;
			SortedList sortedList2 = (SortedList)ApedGlobal.DataManager.ChangedCompounds;
			foreach (IApedChangedCompound value2 in sortedList2.Values)
			{
				IApedChangedCompound val2 = value2;
				if (val2.OldValue == "" || !myCompounds.ContainsThisName(val2.OldValue))
				{
					continue;
				}
				IDbankCompound dbCompound = null;
				myCompounds.GetCompound(val2.OldValue, out dbCompound);
				if (dbCompound != null)
				{
					if (val2.Identifier.Name == "NAME")
					{
						dbCompound.AspenName = val2.NewValue;
					}
					else if (val2.Identifier.Name == "ALIAS")
					{
						dbCompound.AspenName = val2.NewValue;
						dbCompound.AspenAlias = val2.NewValue;
					}
				}
				sortedList.Remove(val2.OldValue);
				sortedList.Add(dbCompound.AspenName, dbCompound);
				if (!flag)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			changedCompoundList = new ArrayList();
			foreach (IDbankCompound value3 in sortedList.Values)
			{
				IDbankCompound value = value3;
				changedCompoundList.Add(value);
			}
		}

		private void GetNewCompoundList(out ArrayList newCompoundList)
		{
			newCompoundList = null;
			myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds = false;
			if (myApedDatabase.NewCompounds != null)
			{
				SortedList sortedList = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.NewCompounds;
				if (sortedList.Count > 0)
				{
					ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList, ref newCompoundList);
				}
			}
			else if (myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds != null)
			{
				SortedList sortedList2 = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds;
				if (sortedList2.Count > 0)
				{
					ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList2, ref newCompoundList);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnDatabaseUpdated(object sender, string databaseName)
		{
			//IL_0089: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds)
			{
				return;
			}
			myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds = false;
			ArrayList arrayList = null;
			if (myApedDatabase.NewCompounds != null)
			{
				SortedList sortedList = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.NewCompounds;
				if (sortedList.Count > 0)
				{
					ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList, ref arrayList);
				}
			}
			else if (myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds != null)
			{
				SortedList sortedList2 = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds;
				if (sortedList2.Count > 0)
				{
					ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList2, ref arrayList);
				}
			}
			if (arrayList != null)
			{
				myCompounds.Adds(arrayList, myNodeType, myNodeName);
			}
		}

		private void updateChildNodes(ApedNodeType nodeType)
		{
			//IL_013c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_013e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0070: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_012c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (mybExpand)
			{
				SortedList sortedList = new SortedList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					sortedList.Add(val.AspenName, val);
				}
				foreach (IDbankCompound value in sortedList.Values)
				{
					IDbankCompound dbc = value;
					try
					{
						UpdateCompoundNodes(dbc);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds)
				{
					myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds = false;
					ArrayList arrayList = null;
					if (myApedDatabase.NewCompounds != null)
					{
						SortedList sortedList2 = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.NewCompounds;
						if (sortedList2.Count > 0)
						{
							ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList2, ref arrayList);
						}
					}
					else if (myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds != null)
					{
						SortedList sortedList3 = (SortedList)myApedDatabase.SelectedCompounds;
						if (sortedList3.Count > 0)
						{
							ApedSharedMethods.ConvertToDbankCompounds(sortedList3, ref arrayList);
						}
					}
					if (arrayList != null)
					{
						myCompounds.Adds(arrayList, myNodeType, myNodeName);
					}
				}
			}
			if (nodeType == myNodeType && ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName)
			{
				ShowCompounds();
			}
		}

		}

		private void UpdateCompoundNodes(IDbankCompound dbc)
		{
			CompoundNode compoundNode = new CompoundNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, this, dbc.AspenName, myDatabank, myCompounds, myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			((BaseNode)compoundNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
		}

		{

		private void OnSelectCompounds(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			if (myApedDatabase.bPropertyValues)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllPropertyValues(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			ApedGlobal.bAdvancedSearch = true;
			ApedGlobal.bDataManager = true;
			if (myApedDatabase.bMW)
			{
				ApedGlobal.bMW = true;
			}
			else
			{
				ApedGlobal.bMW = false;
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bTB)
			{
				ApedGlobal.bTB = true;
			}
			else
			{
				ApedGlobal.bTB = false;
			}
			if (GlobalControl.LocalDB == 6)
			{
				GlobalControl.bReport = true;
				SetRepProp();
			}
			LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg();
			if (GlobalControl.LocalDB == 6)
			{
				GlobalControl.bReport = false;
			}
			ApedGlobal.bAdvancedSearch = false;
			ApedGlobal.bDataManager = false;
			ApedGlobal.bMW = false;
			ApedGlobal.bTB = false;
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private static void SetRepProp()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = (registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APED\\41.0"));
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					object value = registryKey.GetValue("Property");
					if (value != null)
					{
						GlobalControl.reProp = Convert.ToString(value);
					}
					else
					{
						GlobalControl.reProp = string.Empty;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0024: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0036: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedSelectedCompoundsTabCtl val = (ApedSelectedCompoundsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			val.CompoundsRemoved += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnRemoveCompounds);
			val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
			if (val.Database == null)
			{
				val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			SortedList sortedList = new SortedList();
			if (myCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val2 = item;
					try
					{
						sortedList.Add(val2.AspenName, val2);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
			}
			if (sortedList != null && sortedList.Count > 0)
			{
				val.ShowCompounds(sortedList);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void OnRemoveCompounds(object sender, object compounds)
		{
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SortedList sortedList = (SortedList)compounds;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < sortedList.Count; i++)
			{
				arrayList.Add(sortedList.GetByIndex(i));
			}
			myCompounds.Removes(arrayList, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		protected internal void LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg()
		{
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0025: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (dlgSelectCompounds != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			dlgSelectCompounds = new DbankCompoundDialogBox((IFindCompoundLauncher)(object)this);
			if ((int)dlgSelectCompounds.TabDialogControl.OKorCancel == 1)
			{
				if (dlgSelectCompounds != null)
				{
					dlgSelectCompounds = null;
				}

			else if (dlgSelectCompounds != null)
			{
				dlgSelectCompounds = null;
			}
		}

		private void SetWorkingCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myIdentifier == null || myCompounds.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				if (val.Compound == null)
				{
					IIdentifier val2 = null;
					ICompound val3 = null;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(val.AspenName))
					{
						myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref val2);
						myCompoundManager.GetCompound(val.AspenName, val2, ref val3);
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(val.AspenAlias))
					{
						myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("ALIAS", ref val2);
						myCompoundManager.GetCompound(val.AspenAlias, val2, ref val3);
					}
					if (val2 != null && val3 != null)
					{
						val.Compound = val3;
						arrayList.Add(val3);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
			}
			ICollection workingCompounds = arrayList;
			myCompoundManager.SetWorkingCompounds(workingCompounds);
			ApedGlobal.bCompounds = true;
		}

		internal void LaunchManageCompoundsWizard()

			}
		}

		public CompoundsNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0094: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00be: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c4: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
			myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
			if (myApedDatabase.Databanks != null)
			{
				myDatabanks = (IDatabankCollection)myApedDatabase.Databanks;
				ArrayList arrayList = (ArrayList)myApedDatabase.ApedDatabanks;
				foreach (IApedDatabank item in arrayList)
				{
					IApedDatabank val = item;
					if (val.Name == myApedDatabankName)
					{
						myApedDatabank = val;
					}
				}
				if (myCompoundManager == null)
				{
					IDatabase obj = myDatabase;
					ISupportsCompounds val2 = (ISupportsCompounds)(object)((obj is ISupportsCompounds) ? obj : null);
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						myCompoundManager = val2.CompoundManager;
						if (myIdentifier == null)
						{
							myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("NAME", ref myIdentifier);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			Initialize();
			SetCompoundsNodeResultPaneImages(snapin);
		}

		public override void OnSelectScope()
		{
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0067: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnSelectScope();
			((BaseNode)this).Snapin.AddCurrentToolbarsInfo(3);
			if (myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole == 1 && !(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres"))
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList menuItems = ((BaseNode)myNode).m_MenuItems;
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MenuItem item in menuItems)
			{

				}
			}
		}

		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IList list = default(IList);
			dlgSelectCompounds.TabDialogControl.FindCompoundControl.GetSelectedCompounds(ref list);
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in list)
			{
				IDbankCompound dbankCompound = item;
				myCompounds.Add(dbankCompound);
			}
		}

		private bool NewCompound(IDbankCompound dbc)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myListCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				if (dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex == val.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			myListCompounds.Add(dbc);
			if (myApedDatabank.bShow)
			{
				ShowCompounds();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		public DatabankNode(SnapinBase snapin, DatabanksNode parentNode, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, SortedList apedDatabases, IApedMMC dataManager)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ad: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabank = databank;
			myRootCompounds = rootCompounds;
			mySelectedCompounds = new DbankCompoundList();
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
			myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			myDataManager = dataManager;
			ArrayList arrayList = (ArrayList)myApedDatabase.ApedDatabanks;
			if (arrayList != null)
			{
				foreach (IApedDatabank item in arrayList)
				{
					IApedDatabank val = item;
					if (val.Name == myDatabank.Name)
					{
						myApedDatabank = val;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (myDatabase != null && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				mybLocalDB = true;
			}
			myNode = this;
			myNodeDatabanks = parentNode;
			myNodeName = myApedDatabase.Name + myDatabank.Name;
			Initialize();
		}

		public DatabankNode(SnapinBase snapin, DatabanksNode parentNode, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, IApedDatabase apedDatabase)

		protected internal void RemoveThisNode()
		{
			((DatabanksNode)(object)((BaseNode)this).ParentNode).RemoveDatabank(this);
			((BaseNode)this).ParentNode.Children.Remove(this);
			((BaseNode)this).Remove(false);
		}

		}

		private void AddMenuItems()
		{
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_0096: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00aa: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_00bb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres") && (GlobalControl.localDBServer || GlobalControl.runAdmin))
			{
				MenuCommandHandler val = new MenuCommandHandler(OnAddData);

				((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);

				((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);
			}
		}

		public override void OnSelectScope()
		{
			//IL_005f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0065: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnSelectScope();
			((BaseNode)this).Snapin.AddCurrentToolbarsInfo(13);
			if (myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole == 1 && !(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres"))
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList menuItems = ((BaseNode)myNode).m_MenuItems;
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MenuItem item in menuItems)
			{
				MenuItem val = item;
				if (val.Name == "&Delete...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
				if (val.Name == "&Add Data Using Script File...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
				if (val.Name == "&Change Databank Description...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnCreateScript(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			//IL_0208: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0236: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_023d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_02dc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0324: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_032b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			StreamWriter streamWriter = null;
			try
			{
				string ConfigurationFilePath = "";
				ApedApplication.GetConfigurationFilePath(out ConfigurationFilePath);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ConfigurationFilePath))
				{
					MessageBox.Show("Configuration file path was not found where the script file can be stored.", "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					return;
				}
				if (myDatabank == null)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("Databank was not set correctly. Pleae try again.", "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					return;
				}
				int num = ConfigurationFilePath.LastIndexOf('\\');
				ConfigurationFilePath = ConfigurationFilePath.Substring(0, num + 1);
				string fileName = ConfigurationFilePath + myDatabank.Database.Alias + "-" + myDatabank.Name + ".aprdts";
				FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(fileName);
				bool flag = true;
				if (fileInfo.Exists)
				{
					DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("A data transfer script file for this databank already exists.  Do you want to overwrite it?", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
					if (dialogResult == DialogResult.No)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				IDatabase database = myDatabank.Database;
				streamWriter = fileInfo.CreateText();
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Aspen Properties Data Transfer Script File for " + myDatabank.Database.Alias + "-" + myDatabank.Name);
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Options to specify data transfer rules and destination databank information\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<options>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("overwrite-data=no");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("new-databank-name=" + myDatabank.Name);
				streamWriter.WriteLine("new-databank-description=" + myDatabank.Description);
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</options>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Source databases and databanks listed in the order of preference\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<database.databanks>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine(myDatabank.Database.Alias + "." + myDatabank.Name);
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</database.databanks>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Property names for which to transfer property values\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<properties>");
				IPropertyManager propertyManager = ((ISupportsProperties)database).PropertyManager;
				ICollection collection = default(ICollection);
				propertyManager.GetAllProperties(myDatabank, true, ref collection);
				foreach (IProperty item in collection)
				{
					IProperty val = item;
					streamWriter.WriteLine(val.Name);
				}
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</properties>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Compound identifier used in this script file to define compounds\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<identifier>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("ALIAS");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</identifier>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Compounds for which to transfer property values\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<compounds>");
				if (myApedDatabank.Compounds == null)
				{
					ICompoundManager compoundManager = ((ISupportsCompounds)database).CompoundManager;
					ApedUtils.GetAllCompounds(myDatabank, myApedDatabank, compoundManager);
				}
				SortedList sortedList = (SortedList)myApedDatabank.Compounds;
				foreach (DbankCompound value in sortedList.Values)
				{
					DbankCompound val2 = value;
					streamWriter.WriteLine(val2.AspenAlias);
				}
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</compounds>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("; Option to specify whether or not to transfer reaction data\n");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("<reactions>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("transfer-reaction-data=yes");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("</reactions>");
				streamWriter.WriteLine("\n");
				DialogResult dialogResult2 = MessageBox.Show("Aspen Properties data transfer script file : " + myDatabank.Database.Alias + "-" + myDatabank.Name + ".aprdts for this databank has been successfully created in folder : " + ConfigurationFilePath, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				if (streamWriter != null)
				{
					streamWriter.Flush();
					streamWriter.Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnDeleteDatabank(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			//IL_0068: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to permamnently delete Databank " + myDatabank.Name + " in Database " + myDatabase.Alias + "? ", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				RemoveThisNode();
				bool flag = true;
				try
				{
					((Database)myDatabank.Database).DeleteDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					flag = false;
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					flag = false;
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					ApedSharedMethods.GetAllDatabanks(myApedDatabase);
					myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
					ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)12, myApedDatabase.Name);
				}
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		}

		private void LaunchSelectCompoundDlg()
		{
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0025: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4

			{
				return;
			}
			dlgSelectCompounds = new DbankCompoundDialogBox((IFindCompoundLauncher)(object)this);
			if ((int)dlgSelectCompounds.TabDialogControl.OKorCancel == 1)
			{
				if (dlgSelectCompounds != null)
				{
					dlgSelectCompounds = null;
				}
			}
			else if (dlgSelectCompounds != null)
			{
				dlgSelectCompounds = null;
			}
		}

		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedDatabankTabCtl val = (ApedDatabankTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					val.ReleaseExcel();
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}
