		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			mybExpand = true;
			try
			{
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode2 = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode2).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ApedDatabankTabCtl val = (ApedDatabankTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					ShowPropertiesAndParameters(val);
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing databank information: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00bf: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c2: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00d7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00d9: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0034: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					myApedDatabank.bSetup = false;
					ApedDatabankTabCtl val = (ApedDatabankTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowPropertiesAndParameters(val);
						((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 20 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnDatabaseUpdated(object sender, string databaseName)
		{
			if (databaseName == myDatabase.Alias && mybExpand)
			{
				((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
				updateChildNodes();
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedDatabankTabCtl databankTabCtl = (ApedDatabankTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowPropertiesAndParameters(databankTabCtl);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void updateChildNodes()
		{
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0074: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0075: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008f: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			myDatabank.GetContentDataTypes(ref myDataTypes);
			myApedDatabank.bUnary = false;
			myApedDatabank.bBinary = false;
			myApedDatabank.bTernary = false;
			myApedDatabank.bPair = false;
			myApedDatabank.bReaction = false;
			if (myDataTypes != null)
			{
				foreach (ContentDataType myDataType in myDataTypes)
				{
					switch ((int)myDataType)
					{
					case 0:
						myApedDatabank.bUnary = true;
						myDatabank.PureDatabank = true;
						break;
					case 1:
						myApedDatabank.bBinary = true;
						myDatabank.BinaryDatabank = true;
						break;
					case 2:
						myApedDatabank.bTernary = true;
						break;
					case 3:
						myApedDatabank.bPair = true;
						break;
					case 4:
						myApedDatabank.bReaction = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bUnary && !mybPureData)
			{
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myPureDataNode = new PureDataNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabank, myRootCompounds, mySelectedCompounds, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabank, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myPureDataNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
				mybPureData = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bBinary && !mybBinaryData)
			{
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myBinaryDataNode = new BinaryDataNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabank, myRootCompounds, mySelectedCompounds, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabank, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myBinaryDataNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
				mybBinaryData = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bPair && !mybPairData)
			{
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myPairDataNode = new PairDataNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabank, myRootCompounds, mySelectedCompounds, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabank, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myPairDataNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
				mybPairData = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bReaction && !mybReactionData)
			{
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myReactionsDataNode = new ReactionsDataNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabank, myRootCompounds, mySelectedCompounds, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabank, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myReactionsDataNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
				mybReactionData = true;
			}
		}

		private void ShowPropertiesAndParameters(ApedDatabankTabCtl databankTabCtl)
		{
			if (!myApedDatabank.bSetup || myApedDatabank.bChange)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllWithDatabank(myDatabank, myApedDatabank);
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bUnary)
			{
				bPure = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bBinary)
			{
				bBinary = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bTernary)
			{
				bTernary = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bPair)
			{
				bPair = true;
			}
			if (myApedDatabank.bReaction)
			{
				bReaction = true;
			}
			databankTabCtl.SetDatabank(myDatabank, myApedDatabank);
			databankTabCtl.SetTabPages(bPure, bBinary, bTernary, bPair);
		}

		private bool IsCompound(IDbankCompound dbc, ArrayList dbankcompIDs)
		{
			foreach (int dbankcompID in dbankcompIDs)
			{
				string text = dbankcompID.ToString();
				if (text == dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool NewCompound(IDbankCompound dbc)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)mySelectedCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				if (dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex == val.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			mySelectedCompounds.Add(dbc);
			return true;
		}

		internal void LaunchManageCompoundsWizard()
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0020: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedCompoundsWizardForm val = new ApedCompoundsWizardForm(myApedDatabank, myDatabase, myApedDatabases);
			val.FinishClicked += new ApedClickEventHandler(OnFinishClicked);
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			if (val.bCompoundsChanged)
			{
				myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds = true;
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)10, myApedDatabase.Name);
			}
			if (val.bDatabaseChanged)
			{
				ApedSharedMethods.GetAllDatabanks(myApedDatabase);
				myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
				myApedDatabank.bSetup = false;
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)11, myApedDatabase.Name);
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)12, myApedDatabase.Name);
			}
		}

		private void OnFinishClicked(object sender, object changedPropertyList)
		{
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (AddedCompounds == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IDbankCompound AddedCompound in AddedCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound dbankCompound = AddedCompound;
				myRootCompounds.Add(dbankCompound);
			}
		}

	internal class DatabanksNode : FormNode, IApedDatabases
	{
		private IDatabase myDatabase;

		private IDatabank myDatabank;

		private IDatabankCollection myDatabanks;

		private DbankCompoundList myRootCompounds;

		private IApedDatabase myApedDatabase;

		private IApedDatabank myApedDatabank;

		private string myApedDatabankName = "APEDGLOBALDatabanks";

		private SortedList myApedDatabases;

		private ApedNodeType myNodeType = (ApedNodeType)4;

		private string myNodeName = "";

		private IApedMMC myDataManager;

		private bool mybExpand;

		private DatabanksNode myNode;

		private IDatabank myMergeToDbank;

		private int myServerRole = -1;

		private int myDatabaseRole = -1;

		private bool mybLocalDB;

		public string NodeName => myNodeName;

		internal IDatabankCollection SelectedDatabanks => myDatabanks;

		public DatabanksNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, SortedList apedDatabases, IApedMMC dataManager)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
			myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			myRootCompounds = rootCompounds;
			myDataManager = dataManager;
			myNodeName = myApedDatabase.Name + "Databanks";
			myNode = this;
			if (myDatabase != null && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				mybLocalDB = true;
			}
			Initialize();
			SetDatabanksNodeResultPaneImages(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
		}

		public DatabanksNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabase database, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, IApedDatabase apedDatabase)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0085: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00a5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ab: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myServerRole = myApedDatabase.ServerRole;
			myDatabaseRole = myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole;
			myRootCompounds = rootCompounds;
			if (apedDatabase.Databanks != null)
			{
				myDatabanks = (IDatabankCollection)apedDatabase.Databanks;
				ArrayList arrayList = (ArrayList)myApedDatabase.ApedDatabanks;
				foreach (IApedDatabank item in arrayList)
				{
					IApedDatabank val = item;
					if (val.Name == myApedDatabankName)
					{
						myApedDatabank = val;
					}
				}
			}
			if (myDatabase != null && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				mybLocalDB = true;
			}
			Initialize();
			SetDatabanksNodeResultPaneImages(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = "Databanks";
			SetImageIndicesForNode();

		}

		private void AddHandlers()
		{

			myRootCompounds.DatabaseUpdated += OnDatabaseUpdated;
			myApedDatabase.DatabaseUpdate += new ApedDatabaseEventHandler(OnUpdateDatabase);
			myDataManager.DataManagerUpdate += new ApedMMCEventHandler(OnDataManagerUpdate);
		}

		private void AddMenuItems()

			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		}

		public override void OnSelectScope()
		{
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0061: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnSelectScope();
			((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.AddCurrentToolbarsInfo(5);
			if (myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole == 1 && !(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres"))
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList menuItems = ((BaseNode)myNode).m_MenuItems;
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MenuItem item in menuItems)
			{
				MenuItem val = item;
				if (val.Name == "&Create New Databanks...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			//IL_0043: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0049: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0088: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			mybExpand = true;
			try
			{
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				BaseNode val = new BaseNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				val.DisplayName = "(Error)";
				((BaseNode)this).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databanks: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				BaseNode val2 = new BaseNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				val2.DisplayName = "(Error)";
				((BaseNode)this).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowGeneralInfo();
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the databanks information: " + ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00a3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0133: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0135: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowGeneralInfo();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 12 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				if (!mybExpand)
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					myDatabanks = null;
					((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
					updateChildNodes();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex3)
				{
					if (ex3.Message == "")
					{
						string text2 = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text2 != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex3.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnDatabaseUpdated(object sender, string databaseName)
		{
			if (databaseName == myDatabase.Alias && mybExpand)
			{
				myDatabanks = null;
				((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
				updateChildNodes();
				if (ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName)
				{
					ShowGeneralInfo();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				return;

				if ((int)updateType == 5)

					myDatabanks = null;
					((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
					updateChildNodes();
					ShowGeneralInfo();
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();

					{

					}

				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);

			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{

				}
			}
		}

		private void updateChildNodes()
		{
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0097: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0053: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0059: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.ApedDatabanks == null)
			{
				ApedSharedMethods.GetAllDatabanks(myApedDatabase);
			}
			myDatabanks = (IDatabankCollection)myApedDatabase.Databanks;
			if (myDatabanks == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			SortedList sortedList = new SortedList();
			foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)myDatabanks)
			{
				IDatabank val = item;
				sortedList.Add(val.Name, val);
			}
			foreach (IDatabank value in sortedList.Values)
			{
				IDatabank databank = value;
				try
				{
					addDatabankNode(databank);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		private void addDatabankNode(IDatabank databank)
		{
			//IL_0040: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0046: Expected O, but got Unknown
			DatabankNode databankNode = new DatabankNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, this, databank, myRootCompounds, myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			foreach (BaseNode child in ((BaseNode)this).Children)
			{
				BaseNode val = child;
				if (val.DisplayName == databank.Name)
				{
					val.Remove(false);
				}
			}
			((BaseNode)databankNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
		}

		}

				return;

					IDatabank val5 = item3;
					if (val5.Name == item2)
					{
						AddDatabankToSelectedList(val5);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			ArrayList unSelectedStrings = val.UnSelectedStrings;
			foreach (string item4 in unSelectedStrings)
			{

				{
					BaseNode val6 = childNode2;

				}
			}
		}

		internal bool AddDatabankToSelectedList(IDatabank iDbankToAdd)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)myDatabanks)
			{
				IDatabank val = item;
				if (val.Name == iDbankToAdd.Name)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (!((object)myDatabase).Equals((object)iDbankToAdd.Database))
			{
				return false;
			}
			myDatabanks.Add(iDbankToAdd);
			DatabankNode databankNode = new DatabankNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, this, iDbankToAdd, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase);
			((BaseNode)databankNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			return true;
		}

		protected internal void RemoveDatabank(DatabankNode nodeToRemove)
		{

			((BaseNode)this).Children.Remove(nodeToRemove);
		}

		internal void LaunchManageDatabanksWizard(ArrayList RadioButtonsToEnableList)
		{
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0068: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!myApedDatabase.bSetup)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllInformation(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			ApedGlobal.bAbort = false;
			IDatabankCollection val = default(IDatabankCollection);
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val);
			ApedDatabanksWizardForm val2 = new ApedDatabanksWizardForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabases);
			val2.SetDatabankToMergeData(myMergeToDbank);
			val2.EnableRadioButtons(RadioButtonsToEnableList);
			val2.FinishClicked += new ApedClickEventHandler(OnFinishClicked);
			GetUserPassword(myDatabase, out var userPassword);
			if (userPassword != null && userPassword != "")
			{
				val2.UserPassword = userPassword;
			}
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val2).ShowDialog();
			if (ApedGlobal.bAbort)
			{
				myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)8, "");
				return;
			}
			if (val2.bCompoundsChanged)
			{
				myApedDatabase.bNewCompounds = true;
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)10, myApedDatabase.Name);
			}
			myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
			ApedSharedMethods.GetAllDatabanks(myApedDatabase);
			myApedDatabases[myApedDatabase.Name] = myApedDatabase;
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)12, myApedDatabase.Name);
		}

		public void CreateNewDatabase(string databaseType, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string creator_Pwd, out IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			database = null;
			try
			{
				if (databaseType.CompareTo("SQL Server") == 0)
				{
					SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
					string text = "";
					string text2 = "";
					val.CreateNew(databaseName, serverName, userName, userPassword, databaseName, text, text2, creator_Pwd);
					database = (IDatabase)(object)val;
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show("This function is not supported for Oracle databases", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred creating the database: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

			{

				{
					myApedDatabase = val;
				}

		}

					myCompoundManager = val.CompoundManager;

			}

					IDatabaseObject item = ((ISupportsSundryData)database).SundryDataManager.GetItem(sundryDataTableEntryName);

			//IL_0032: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_007c: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_008d: Expected O, but got Unknown

					((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);

		private void onToolbarClickEventHandler(object sender, NodeEventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			switch (((CommandArgs)args).m_command)
			{
			case 4:
				RemoveThisNode();
				break;
			case 3:
				ApedGlobal.bAdvancedSearch = true;
				LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg();
				break;
			case 6:
				ShowUserOptionsDialog();
				break;
			case 5:
				break;
			}
		}

		private bool bHideItem()
		{
			string text = myDatabase.DataSource.ToUpperInvariant();
			bool result = true;
			if (text.Contains(Environment.MachineName.ToUpperInvariant()) || text.Contains("SQLEXPRESS"))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string DropDBWithLocalDB()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				RemoveThisNode();
				return APEDSMOGlobal.DropDatabase(myDatabase.DataSource, "", "", myDatabase.Alias);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				return ex.Message;
			}
		}

		private void OnDeleteDatabase(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to permanently delete Database " + myDatabase.Alias + "? ", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult != DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				return;
			}
			string empty = string.Empty;
			bool flag = false;
			if (!((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(myApedDatabase.Password)) ? APEDSMOGlobal.CanTheCurrentUserDropDatabase(myDatabase.DataSource, myApedDatabase.UserName, myApedDatabase.Password) : APEDSMOGlobal.CanTheCurrentUserDropDatabase(myDatabase.DataSource, "", "")))
			{
				empty = "Failed to delete Database " + myDatabase.Alias + " permanently from the server " + myDatabase.DataSource + ". You don't have permission to drop this database from the server.";
				MessageBox.Show(empty, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				return;
			}
			RemoveThisNode();
			empty = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(myApedDatabase.Password)) ? APEDSMOGlobal.DropDatabase(myDatabase.DataSource, myApedDatabase.UserName, myApedDatabase.Password, myDatabase.Alias) : APEDSMOGlobal.DropDatabase(myDatabase.DataSource, "", "", myDatabase.Alias));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
			{
				empty = "Failed to delete Database " + myDatabase.Alias + " permanently from the server " + myDatabase.DataSource + ". You don't have permission to drop this database from the server.";
				MessageBox.Show(empty, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			else if (myApedDatabases.Count == 0)
			{
				string ConfigurationFilePath = string.Empty;
				ApedApplication.GetConfigurationFilePath(out ConfigurationFilePath);
				empty = ApedApplication.CanTheFileBeDeleted(ConfigurationFilePath);
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void OnImportInputFile(object obj, BaseNode node)

			ImportInputFile importInputFile = new ImportInputFile(myDatabase);
			importInputFile.ShowDialog();
		}

		private string DeleteDBWithSQLMode(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string result = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).Delete1();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				result = ((ex.GetBaseException() == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(ex.GetBaseException().Message)) ? ((ex.InnerException != null) ? ex.InnerException.Message : ex.Message) : ex.GetBaseException().Message);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string DeleteDBWithWindowsMode(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			string result = string.Empty;

			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).Delete();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				result = ((ex.GetBaseException() == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(ex.GetBaseException().Message)) ? ((ex.InnerException != null) ? ex.InnerException.Message : ex.Message) : ex.GetBaseException().Message);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal void LaunchViewChangesForm()

				((Form)(object)val2).ShowDialog();

			else
			{
				MessageBox.Show("Currently there are no pending changes", "Clear Changes");
			}
		}

		private void onEditRegistrationCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			//IL_0106: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_010d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabase.Version != 0)
			{
				DatabasePropertiesForm databasePropertiesForm = new DatabasePropertiesForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				databasePropertiesForm.ShowDialog();
				return;
			}
			RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes databaseTypes = ((!(myDatabase is SqlServerDatabase)) ? RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes.Oracle : RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes.SqlServer);
			RegisteredDatabaseForm registeredDatabaseForm = new RegisteredDatabaseForm(myApedDatabases, myDatabase);
			registeredDatabaseForm.Text = "Database Registration Properties";
			registeredDatabaseForm.AliasIsReadOnly = true;
			registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias.Add(myDatabase.Alias);
			registeredDatabaseForm.ServerName = myDatabase.DataSource;
			registeredDatabaseForm.Username = myDatabase.UserName;
			registeredDatabaseForm.bEdit = true;
			if (myDatabase is SqlServerDatabase)
			{
				ArrayList initialCatalog = registeredDatabaseForm.InitialCatalog;
				IDatabase obj2 = myDatabase;
				initialCatalog.Add(((SqlServerDatabase)((obj2 is SqlServerDatabase) ? obj2 : null)).InitialCatalog);
			}
			if (registeredDatabaseForm.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				string text = null;
				if (registeredDatabaseForm.PasswordChanged)
				{
					text = registeredDatabaseForm.Password;
				}
				IDatabase val = null;
				if (registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseType == databaseTypes)
				{
					val = myDatabase;
				}
				else if (registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseType == RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes.SqlServer)
				{
					val = (IDatabase)new SqlServerDatabase();
				}
				registeredDatabaseForm.Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				try
				{
					if (val is SqlServerDatabase)
					{
						((SqlServerDatabase)((val is SqlServerDatabase) ? val : null)).Connect(registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias[0].ToString(), registeredDatabaseForm.ServerName, registeredDatabaseForm.Username, text, registeredDatabaseForm.InitialCatalog[0].ToString());
					}
					myDatabase = val;
					((BaseNode)this).Children.Clear();
					((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("An error occurred updating the database registration properties: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				finally
				{
					registeredDatabaseForm.Cursor = Cursors.Default;
				}
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void onUpgradeDatabaseCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("Based on the size of your database it may take several hours to upgrade it. Are you sure you want to upgrade your database from Version 2006 to Version 2006.5 ? ", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				try
				{
					LaunchUpgradeDatabaseWizard();
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message);
				}
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)8, "");
		}

		private void onDeleteRegistrationCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("Are you sure you want to delete registration for " + myDatabase.Alias + "? ", "APED Warning", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				if (GlobalControl.Switch)
				{
					GlobalControl.Switch = false;
				}
				RemoveThisNode();
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			if (!myApedDatabase.bMWTB)
			{
				if (GlobalControl.APNames.Contains(myApedDatabase.Name.ToUpperInvariant()))
				{
					myApedDatabase.bMW = true;
					if (GlobalControl.APNoTBNames.Contains(myApedDatabase.Name.ToUpperInvariant()))
					{
						myApedDatabase.bTB = false;
					}
					else
					{
						myApedDatabase.bTB = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ApedUtils.GetAllWithDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				myApedDatabase.bMWTB = true;
			}
			try
			{
				if (myServerRole == -1 && !mybLocalDB)
				{
					GetRolesForThisDB();
				}
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode2 = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode2).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				if (myServerRole == -1 && !mybLocalDB)
				{
					GetRolesForThisDB();
				}
				ShowGeneral();
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the database information: " + ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a0: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b7: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowGeneral();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 9 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void ShowGeneral()
		{
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.Database != null && !myApedDatabase.Database.DataManagerAccess)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("You do not have permission to view databank contents in this database using Database Manager.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			ApedDatabaseCtl val = (ApedDatabaseCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.IsReadOnly = true;
				val.SetApedDatabase(myApedDatabase);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_003a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedDatabaseCtl val = (ApedDatabaseCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				val.IsReadOnly = true;
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					val.SetApedDatabase(myApedDatabase);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void updateChildNodes()
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0040: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myCompoundsNode = new CompoundsNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			((BaseNode)myCompoundsNode).OnClickEvent += new NodeNotificationHandler(myCompoundsNode_OnClickEvent);
			((BaseNode)myCompoundsNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			myPropertiesAndParametersNode = new PropertiesAndParametersNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myCompoundsNode.SelectedCompounds, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			((BaseNode)myPropertiesAndParametersNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			myDatabanksNode = new DatabanksNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myCompoundsNode.SelectedCompounds, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			((BaseNode)myDatabanksNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
		}

		public override void OnRefresh()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((BaseNode)this).OnRefresh();
		}

		public void RemoveThisNode()
		{
			if (!GlobalControl.Switch)
			{
				((DatabasesNode)(object)((BaseNode)this).ParentNode).RemoveDatabaseFromSelection(myDatabase);
			}
			((BaseNode)this).ParentNode.Children.Remove(this);
			((BaseNode)this).Remove(false);
		}

		public void RemoveThisNode(IDatabase database)
		{
			if (!GlobalControl.Switch)
			{
				((DatabasesNode)(object)((BaseNode)this).ParentNode).RemoveDatabaseFromSelection(database);
			}
			((BaseNode)this).ParentNode.Children.Remove(this);
			((BaseNode)this).Remove(false);
		}

		private void RestoreThisNode()
		{
			((DatabasesNode)(object)((BaseNode)this).ParentNode).RestoreDatabaseFromSelection(myDatabase);
		}

		internal void LaunchUpgradeDatabaseWizard()
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedUpgradeDatabaseForm val = new ApedUpgradeDatabaseForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			string databaseType = "SQL Server";
			GetLocalServerNames(databaseType, out var localServerNames);
			if (localServerNames != null)
			{
				val.LocalServerNames = localServerNames;
			}
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
		}

		internal void LaunchUpdateDatabaseWizard()
		{
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00d5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ApedGlobal.bAbort = false;
			IDatabase val = myDatabase;
			IDatabankCollection val2 = default(IDatabankCollection);
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref val2);
			ApedUpdateDatabaseForm val3 = new ApedUpdateDatabaseForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			string databaseType = "SQL Server";
			GetLocalServerNames(databaseType, out var localServerNames);
			if (localServerNames != null)
			{
				val3.LocalServerNames = localServerNames;
			}
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val3).ShowDialog();
			if (!ApedGlobal.bAbort)
			{
				if (!val3.bDatabaseChanged)
				{
					return;
				}
				myApedDatabase.bSetup = false;
				IDatabase updateDatabase = null;
				UpdateDatabase(myApedDatabase.Name, myApedDatabase.UserName, myApedDatabase.Password, myApedDatabase.ServerType, myApedDatabase.ServerName, out updateDatabase);
				if (updateDatabase != null)
				{
					myDatabase = updateDatabase;
					myApedDatabase.Database = myDatabase;
					((Database)(SqlServerDatabase)myDatabase).ConfirmApplicationPwd("ATVIEWER");
					ApedSharedMethods.GetAllDatabanks(myApedDatabase);
					if (myApedDatabases.ContainsKey(myApedDatabase.Name))
					{
						myApedDatabases[myApedDatabase.Name] = myApedDatabase;
					}
				}
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)8, "");
			}
			else if (val != null)
			{
				RemoveThisNode(val);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDatabase(string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string databaseType, string serverName, out IDatabase updateDatabase)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			updateDatabase = null;
			if (databaseType == "SQL Server")
			{
				SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
				if (val.Connect(databaseName, serverName, userName, userPassword, databaseName))
				{
					updateDatabase = (IDatabase)(object)val;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ContainThisDatabank(IDatabankCollection databanks, IDatabank thisDatabank)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (IDatabank item in (IEnumerable)databanks)
			{
				IDatabank val = item;
				if (val.Name == thisDatabank.Name)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void DeleteDatabaseIfNotInUse(IDatabase database, out string message)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			message = "";
			try
			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).DeleteDatabaseIfNotInUse();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				message = ex.Message;
			}
		}

		public bool SetEditMode()
		{
			if (myDatabaseRole == 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool SetDeleteMode()
		{
			if (myServerName.Contains("pipe") && myServerName.Contains("tsql") && myServerName.Contains("query"))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!ApedApplication.IsLocalServerInstance(myServerName))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (myServerRole == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void GetRolesForThisDB()
		{
			myServerRole = 3;
			myDatabaseRole = 3;
			if (myDatabase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			myServerName = myDatabase.DataSource;
			if (myServerName.Contains("pipe") && myServerName.Contains("tsql") && myServerName.Contains("query"))
			{
				myServerRole = 0;
				myDatabaseRole = 1;
			}
			else if (GlobalControl.runAdmin)
			{
				int serverRole = 3;
				string text = APEDSMOGlobal.CheckLogin(myServerName, GlobalControl.currentLogin, out serverRole);
				if (serverRole == 0)
				{
					myServerRole = serverRole;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(myApedDatabase.Password))
				{
					int serverRole2 = 3;
					int roleType = 0;
					text = APEDSMOGlobal.GetDatabaseRole(myServerName, myApedDatabase.Name, myApedDatabase.UserName, myApedDatabase.Password, out serverRole2, out myDatabaseRole, out roleType);
					if (serverRole2 < serverRole)
					{
						myServerRole = serverRole2;
					}
					else
					{
						myServerRole = serverRole;
					}
				}
			}
			if ((GlobalControl.APNames.Contains(myApedDatabase.Name.ToUpperInvariant()) && GlobalControl.LocalDB <= 5) || !myApedDatabase.bView)
			{
				myDatabaseRole = 3;
			}
			else if (myDatabaseRole != 1 && myServerRole <= 1)
			{
				myDatabaseRole = 1;
			}
			myApedDatabase.ServerRole = myServerRole;
			myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole = myDatabaseRole;
		}

		public void GetServerRoleForThisDB()
		{
			myServerRole = 3;
			if (myDatabase == null || !GlobalControl.runAdmin)
			{
				return;
			}
			int serverRole = 3;
			string text = APEDSMOGlobal.CheckLogin(myServerName, GlobalControl.currentLogin, out serverRole);
			if (serverRole > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(myApedDatabase.Password))
			{
				int serverRole2 = 3;
				text = APEDSMOGlobal.CheckLogin(myServerName, myApedDatabase.UserName, myApedDatabase.Password, out serverRole2);
				if (serverRole2 < serverRole)
				{
					myServerRole = serverRole2;
				}
				else
				{
					myServerRole = serverRole;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				myServerRole = serverRole;
			}
			myApedDatabase.ServerRole = myServerRole;
		}

		public void CreateNewDatabase(string databaseType, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string creator_Pwd, out IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			database = null;
			try
			{
				if (databaseType.CompareTo("SQL Server") == 0)
				{
					SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
					string text = "";
					string text2 = "";
					val.CreateNew(databaseName, serverName, userName, userPassword, databaseName, text, text2, creator_Pwd);
					database = (IDatabase)(object)val;
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show("This function is not supported for Oracle databases", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred creating the database: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		public void RegisterDatabase(string databaseType, string databaseAlias, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, out IDatabase database)
		{
			database = null;
		}

		public void DeleteDatabase(IDatabase database)
		{
			string alias = database.Alias;
			string value = DeleteDBWithSQLMode(database);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				value = DeleteDBWithWindowsMode(database);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					string text = "Failed to delete Database " + alias + " permanently from the server " + database.DataSource + ". You don't have permission to drop this database.";
					MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

	public class DatabasePropertiesForm : Form
	{
		public enum DatabaseTypes
		{
			SqlServer,
			Oracle
		}

		private IContainer components;

		private TextBox textBoxDatabaseType;

		private Label label1;

		private Label label2;

		private Label label3;

		private Label label4;

		private Label label5;

		private Label label6;

		private TextBox textBoxServer;

		private TextBox textBoxUserName;

		private TextBox textBoxPassword;

		private TextBox textBoxDatabaseName;

		private TextBox textBoxDatabaseVersion;

		private Button buttonClose;

		private IDatabase myDatabase;

		private IApedDatabase myApedDatabase;

		private string myCurrentVersion = "";

		private string myNewVersion = "";

		private string myDllVersion = "";

		private string myCurrentBuild = "";

		private string myNewBuild = "";

		private bool myFormClose;

		private SortedList m_Controls = new SortedList();

		private Rectangle m_initBounds;

		private Rectangle m_prevBounds;

		private Label labelVersion;

		private TextBox textBoxVersion;

		private Label labelbuild;

		private TextBox textBoxChange;

		private CheckBox checkBoxChange;

		private ToolTip toolTipChangeBuild;

		private Label labelOwner;

		private TextBox textBoxOwner;

		public DatabasePropertiesForm()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public DatabasePropertiesForm(IDatabase database, IApedDatabase apedDatabase)
		{
			//IL_0132: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0139: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabase = database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			InitializeComponent();
			myCurrentVersion = myDatabase.DataVersion;
			int version = myDatabase.Version;
			string alias = myDatabase.Alias;
			if (version < 24)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetVersionAndBuildNumber(version, alias, ref myDllVersion, ref myCurrentBuild);
				myCurrentVersion = myDllVersion + myCurrentBuild;
			}
			else
			{
				myDllVersion = myCurrentVersion.Substring(0, 7);
				myCurrentBuild = myCurrentVersion.Substring(7, myCurrentVersion.Length - 7);
			}
			m_initBounds = base.Bounds;
			m_prevBounds = m_initBounds;
			if (base.Controls == null || base.Controls.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Control control in base.Controls)
			{
				IChildControl value = (IChildControl)new ChildControl((Control)this, control);
				m_Controls.Add(control.Name, value);
			}
		}

		public void ResizeAllControls(int width, int height)
		{
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (m_Controls != null && m_Controls.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IChildControl value in m_Controls.Values)
				{
					IChildControl val = value;
					Control child = val.Child;
					int num = (int)(val.X * (double)width);
					int num2 = (int)(val.Y * (double)height);
					int num3 = (int)(val.W * (double)width);
					int num4 = (int)(val.H * (double)height);
					child.SetBounds(num, num2, num3, num4);
				}
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		private void DatabasePropertiesForm_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ResizeAllControls(base.ClientSize.Width, base.ClientSize.Height);
			myFormClose = false;
			textBoxDatabaseType.Text = "SQL Server";
			if (myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && myDatabase.DataSource.Contains("query"))
			{
				label2.Text = "Local DB Server";
				textBoxServer.Text = GlobalControl.localSQLShareName + " (" + GlobalControl.LocalDBVersionUsed + ")";
				labelOwner.Visible = true;
				textBoxOwner.Text = GlobalControl.currentLogin;
				textBoxOwner.Visible = true;
				textBoxOwner.Enabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				textBoxServer.Text = myDatabase.DataSource;
				labelOwner.Visible = false;
				textBoxOwner.Visible = false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(myDatabase.UserName))
			{
				textBoxUserName.Text = myDatabase.UserName;
				textBoxPassword.Text = "**********";
			}
			else
			{
				textBoxUserName.Text = "";
				textBoxPassword.Text = "";
			}
			textBoxDatabaseName.Text = myDatabase.Alias;
			int version = myDatabase.Version;
			SortedList aPVersions = GlobalControl.APVersions;
			textBoxDatabaseVersion.Text = aPVersions[version].ToString();
			textBoxVersion.Text = myCurrentVersion;
			textBoxChange.Text = myCurrentBuild;
			textBoxDatabaseType.Enabled = false;
			textBoxServer.Enabled = false;
			textBoxUserName.Enabled = false;
			textBoxPassword.Enabled = false;
			textBoxDatabaseName.Enabled = false;
			textBoxDatabaseVersion.Enabled = false;
			textBoxVersion.Enabled = false;
			textBoxChange.Enabled = false;
			checkBoxChange.Checked = false;
			if (myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole != 1 || myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres")
			{
				checkBoxChange.Visible = false;
			}
		}

		private void buttonClose_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!myFormClose)
			{
				myFormClose = true;
				myNewVersion = textBoxVersion.Text.Trim();
				if (myNewVersion.CompareTo(myCurrentVersion) != 0)
				{
					myDatabase.UpdateDatabaseVersion(myNewVersion);
				}
				Close();
			}
		}

		private void textBoxChange_TextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (textBoxChange.Text.Trim() != "")
			{
				string text = textBoxChange.Text.Trim();
				if (text.StartsWith("0"))
				{
					if (text.Length > 1)
					{
						textBoxChange.Text = text.Substring(1, text.Length - 1);
						myNewBuild = textBoxChange.Text.Trim();
					}
					else
					{
						textBoxChange.Text = "";
						myNewBuild = myCurrentBuild;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					myNewBuild = textBoxChange.Text.Trim();
				}
				textBoxVersion.Text = myDllVersion + myNewBuild;
			}
			else
			{
				textBoxVersion.Text = myCurrentVersion;
			}
		}

		private void textBoxChange_KeyPress(object sender, KeyPressEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsRealNumFormat(e.KeyChar))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private bool IsRealNumFormat(char keyChar)
		{
			switch (keyChar)
			{
			case '\b':
			case '0':
			case '1':
			case '2':
			case '3':
			case '4':
			case '5':
			case '6':
			case '7':
			case '8':
			case '9':
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void checkBoxChange_CheckedChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (checkBoxChange.Checked)
			{
				textBoxChange.Enabled = true;
				toolTipChangeBuild.SetToolTip(checkBoxChange, "Change Build Number");
			}
			else
			{
				textBoxChange.Enabled = false;
				toolTipChangeBuild.SetToolTip(checkBoxChange, "Lock Build Number");
			}
		}

		private void DatabasePropertiesForm_FormClosed(object sender, FormClosedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void DatabasePropertiesForm_FormClosing(object sender, FormClosingEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!myFormClose)
			{
				myFormClose = true;
				myNewVersion = textBoxVersion.Text.Trim();
				if (myNewVersion.CompareTo(myCurrentVersion) != 0)
				{
					myDatabase.UpdateDatabaseVersion(myNewVersion);
				}
				Close();
			}
		}

				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		public DatabasesNode(SnapinBase snapin, out SortedList apedDatabases, out SortedList newDatabases)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_004d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Expected O, but got Unknown
			apedDatabases = myApedDatabases;
			newDatabases = myNewDatabases;
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = "Database Manager - Aspen Properties V15";
			myNode = this;
			GlobalControl.bDataManager = true;
			ApedGlobal.DataManager = myDataManager;
			string text = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
			string text2 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";
			string text3 = text.ToUpper();
			string text4 = text2.ToLower();
			text3 = text.ToUpperInvariant();
			text4 = text2.ToLowerInvariant();
			if (myNumInfo == null)
			{
				myNumInfo = NumberFormatInfo.CurrentInfo;
				GlobalControl.decimalSeparator = myNumInfo.CurrencyDecimalSeparator;
				GlobalControl.groupSeparator = myNumInfo.CurrencyGroupSeparator;
			}
			ApedApplication.GetAspenDBAndSkip();
			_ = GlobalControl.Next;
			_ = 5;
			if (ApedApplication.GetDBProvider().CompareTo("postgres") == 0)
			{
				string text5 = "You currently select PostgreSQL as Database Provider. You can switch to SQL Server using Database Configuration Tool.";
				MessageBox.Show(text5, "Aspen Properties Database Manager for SQL Server", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.Destroy();
				return;
			}
			GlobalControl.isAPlusRun = false;
			string configfilepath = "";
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (!(myDBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres"))
			{
				empty = ApedApplication.ConfigFileCheck(out configfilepath);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
				{
					myDBRestore = true;
				}
				else if (empty.CompareTo("OK") != 0)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(configfilepath + empty, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				if (GlobalControl.localDBServer && GlobalControl.bMMCFirst)
				{
					string text6 = "Aspen Properties system databases are not restored with SqlLocalDB yet. Click OK to restore the databases. Click Cancel to quit.";
					DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show(text6, "Database Manager not available", MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					if (dialogResult == DialogResult.OK)
					{
						StartDatabaseTester();
					}
					((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.Destroy();
					return;
				}
			}
			Initialize();
			if (SLMCheckoutLicense())
			{
				mybSLMCheck = false;
				if (GlobalControl.sysRestored)
				{
					if (GlobalControl.restoredDatabases.CompareTo(GlobalControl.sysDatabases) == 0)
					{
						InitializeDatabasesFromDefaultConfig();
						GlobalControl.sysRestored = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					InitializeDatabasesFromDefaultConfig();
				}
				GetServerName();
				UpdateAllDatabases();
			}
			else
			{
				if (mybCancel)
				{
					((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.Destroy();
					return;
				}
				if (!mybCancel)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(licenseErr, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.Destroy();
					return;
				}
				mybSLMCheck = true;
			}
			SetLicMgrString();
		}

		private void StartDatabaseTester()
		{
			string fileName = GlobalControl.apedtop + "\\dbTesterAdv.exe";
			ProcessStartInfo processStartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo();
			processStartInfo.FileName = fileName;
			processStartInfo.CreateNoWindow = true;
			processStartInfo.WindowStyle = ProcessWindowStyle.Normal;
			Process.Start(processStartInfo);
		}

		private void StartDatabaseSelector()
		{
			string fileName = GlobalControl.apedtop + "\\setaped.exe";
			ProcessStartInfo processStartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo();
			processStartInfo.FileName = fileName;
			processStartInfo.CreateNoWindow = true;
			processStartInfo.WindowStyle = ProcessWindowStyle.Normal;
			Process.Start(processStartInfo);
		}

		private void StartDBRestore()
		{
			string fileName = GlobalControl.apedtop + "\\dbRestore.exe";
			ProcessStartInfo processStartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo();
			processStartInfo.FileName = fileName;
			processStartInfo.CreateNoWindow = true;
			processStartInfo.WindowStyle = ProcessWindowStyle.Normal;
			Process.Start(processStartInfo);
		}

		private void UpdateAllDatabases()
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00e2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00e9: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0098: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabases == null || ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
			{
				IDatabase val = item;
				int databaseVersion = val.DatabaseVersion;
				int version = val.Version;
				if (GlobalControl.bFIPS && version < 34)
				{
					arrayList.Add(val);
				}
			}
			if (arrayList.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDatabase item2 in arrayList)
				{
					IDatabase val2 = item2;
					myDatabases.Remove(val2);
				}
			}
			foreach (IDatabase item3 in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
			{
				IDatabase val3 = item3;
				AddApedDatabase(val3, val3.UserNameByReg, val3.PasswordByReg, out var apedDatabase);
				if (apedDatabase != null && !myApedDatabases.Contains(apedDatabase.Name))
				{
					myApedDatabases.Add(apedDatabase.Name, apedDatabase);
				}
			}
		}

		private void GetServerName()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(GlobalControl.serverName))
			{
				GlobalControl.machineName = Environment.MachineName.ToUpperInvariant();
				if (GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances == null && GlobalControl.AspenDB != 2)
				{
					ApedApplication.GetAllSQLInstances();
				}
				if (GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances != null && GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances.Count > 0)
				{
					string sqlInstanceName = string.Empty;
					GetSelectedSQLServerInstance(out sqlInstanceName);
					string[] array = GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances[sqlInstanceName].ToString().Split(';');
					GlobalControl.isSysAdminH = Convert.ToInt32(array[6]);
					GlobalControl.serverName = APEDSMOGlobal.GetServerName(sqlInstanceName);
				}
				else
				{
					GlobalControl.serverName = "Enter SQL Server Instance";
				}
			}
		}

		private void GetSelectedSQLServerInstance(out string sqlInstanceName)
		{
			sqlInstanceName = string.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			if (GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances == null || GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances.Keys)
			{
				string[] array = GlobalControl.listOfSQLInstances[key].ToString().Split(';');
				if (Convert.ToInt32(array[5]) > num)
				{
					sqlInstanceName = key;
					num = Convert.ToInt32(array[5]);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool CheckLocalSQL(string serverName)
		{
			return ApedApplication.LinkingToSQLServer(serverName, "", "");
		}

		private void SetLicMgrString()
		{
			if (GlobalControl.bCompanyLicMgrStr || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(GlobalControl.CompanyLicMgrStr))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (GlobalControl.Next > 0)
			{
				GlobalControl.bCompanyLicMgrStr = true;
				GlobalControl.CompanyLicMgrStr = "ASPENTECH LTD";
				return;
			}
			GlobalControl.bCompanyLicMgrStr = true;
			string text = "";
			int num = -1;
			int infover = -1;
			string text2 = "";
			try
			{
				SecServer secServer = (SecServer)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("1460FDB4-752B-11D4-B067-0090271CF383")));
				if (secServer != null)
				{
					ISecCallback lostLicenseCallback = null;
					LicenseImportance_enum importance = LicenseImportance_enum.liCritical;
					text2 = secServer.CheckoutLicense("SLM_LicenseProfile", "", out num, lostLicenseCallback, importance);
					if (num != 0)
					{
						text = secServer.InfoString(num, out infover);
						secServer.ReleaseLicense(num);
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					GlobalControl.CompanyLicMgrStr = text.Substring(36);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				text = ex.Message;
			}
		}

		private bool SLMCheckoutLicense()
		{
			return true;
		}

		private void AppInitCheckout()
		{
			if (appinit == null)
			{
				appinit = (AppDataInitObject)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("6B68DC8F-4D15-4A9C-A3B9-97B8EB54D1BA")));
			}
			if (appinit == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool binit = false;
			string errorstring = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < GlobalControl.AspenLicensedProducts.Count; i++)
			{
				string license = GlobalControl.AspenLicensedProducts[i].ToString();
				appinit.appdatainit(license, out binit, out errorstring);
				if (binit)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void ReleaseLicense()
		{
			if (cookie != 0)
			{
				secServer.ReleaseLicense(cookie);
				cookie = 0;
			}
			if (cookieProfile != 0)
			{
				secServer.ReleaseLicense(cookieProfile);
				cookieProfile = 0;
			}
			if (secServer != null)
			{
				secServer = null;
			}
			if (appinit != null)
			{
				appinit = null;
			}
		}

			AddHandlers();
			AddMenuItems();
			((FormNode)this).ControlType = typeof(ApedDatabasesCtl);
		}

		private void onToolbarClickEventHandler(object sender, NodeEventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			switch (((CommandArgs)args).m_command)
			{
			case 1:
				InitializeDatabasesFromAnyConfig();
				break;
			case 7:
				MessageBox.Show("This command not yet implemented");
				break;
			}
		}

		public override void OnSelectScope()
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnSelectScope();
			((BaseNode)this).SetVerbState((MMC_CONSOLE_VERB)32774, (MMC_BUTTON_STATE)1, true);
			((BaseNode)this).Snapin.AddCurrentToolbarsInfo(1);
			ArrayList menuItems = ((BaseNode)myNode).m_MenuItems;
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MenuItem item in menuItems)
			{
				MenuItem val = item;
				string name = val.Name;
				if (name == "&Check out a license...")
				{
					if (mybSLMCheck)
					{
						val.Visible = true;
					}
					else
					{
						val.Visible = false;
					}
				}
				else if (mybSLMCheck)
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
				else
				{
					val.Visible = true;
				}
				if (name == "&Configure SQL Server...")
				{
					if (ApedApplication.AnySQLForConfiguration())
					{
						val.Visible = true;
					}
					else
					{
						val.Visible = false;
					}
				}
				if (name == "&Update Aspen Properties system databases...")
				{
					if (GlobalControl.localDBServer && !GlobalControl.localDBUpdate)
					{
						val.Visible = true;
					}
					else
					{
						val.Visible = false;
					}
				}
				if (name == "&Switch from SqlLocalDB to SQL Server...")
				{
					if (ApedApplication.AnySQLForConfiguration() && GlobalControl.runAdmin && GlobalControl.localDBServer && GlobalControl.isWinPC && myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0 && !GlobalControl.Switch)
					{
						val.Visible = true;
					}
					else
					{
						val.Visible = false;
					}
				}
				if (name == "&Switch from SQL Server to SqlLocalDB...")
				{
					if (GlobalControl.Switch && GlobalControl.runAdmin && GlobalControl.isWinPC)
					{
						val.Visible = true;
					}
					else
					{
						val.Visible = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnShowSecurityStringCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (!GlobalControl.bCompanyLicMgrStr)
			{
				int num = -1;
				int infover = -1;
				string text2 = "";
				try
				{
					SecServer secServer = (SecServer)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("1460FDB4-752B-11D4-B067-0090271CF383")));
					if (secServer != null)
					{
						ISecCallback lostLicenseCallback = null;
						LicenseImportance_enum importance = LicenseImportance_enum.liCritical;
						text2 = secServer.CheckoutLicense("SLM_LicenseProfile", "", out num, lostLicenseCallback, importance);
						if (num != 0)
						{
							text = secServer.InfoString(num, out infover);
							secServer.ReleaseLicense(num);
						}
					}
					GlobalControl.bCompanyLicMgrStr = true;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						MessageBox.Show("Database security string for your company is: \n\n" + (GlobalControl.CompanyLicMgrStr = text.Substring(36)), "Database security string", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show("An error occurred while retrieving database security string.", "Database security string", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch
				{
					MessageBox.Show("An error occurred while retrieving database security string.", "Database security string", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
					return;
				}
			}
			text = GlobalControl.CompanyLicMgrStr;
			MessageBox.Show("Database security string for your company is: \n\n" + text, "Database security string", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
		}
