		private IDatabase APEDDatabase(string dbName, string serverName, string userName, string userPassword, string initialCatalog)
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IDatabase val = null;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				SqlServerDatabase val2 = new SqlServerDatabase();
				val2.Connect(dbName, serverName, userName, userPassword, initialCatalog);
				val = (IDatabase)(object)val2;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				empty = ex.Message;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				empty = ex2.Message;
			}
			return val;
		}

		private void onNewRegistrationCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			string lastServerName = ((myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0) ? myDatabases[((ICollection)myDatabases).Count - 1].DataSource : "");
			string lastUserName = ((myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0) ? myDatabases[((ICollection)myDatabases).Count - 1].UserName : "");
			RegisteredDatabaseForm registeredDatabaseForm = new RegisteredDatabaseForm(myApedDatabases, lastServerName, lastUserName);
			using (registeredDatabaseForm)
			{
				if (registeredDatabaseForm.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
				{
					return;
				}
				registeredDatabaseForm.Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				try
				{
					if (DoesDatabaseNameExist(registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias) && MessageBox.Show("One or more of the selected database names already exists.  Would you like to overwrite?", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation) == DialogResult.No)
					{
						return;
					}
					string userName = null;
					string userPassword = null;
					if (registeredDatabaseForm.Username != "")
					{
						userName = registeredDatabaseForm.Username;
						userPassword = registeredDatabaseForm.Password;
					}
					IDatabase val = null;
					ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
					ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList();
					ArrayList arrayList3 = new ArrayList();
					ArrayList arrayList4 = new ArrayList();
					int num = 0;
					string text = string.Empty;
					for (int i = 0; i < registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias.Count; i++)
					{
						val = (IDatabase)((!registeredDatabaseForm.IDatabaseList.Contains((string)registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias[i])) ? null : /*isinst with value type is only supported in some contexts*/);
						if (val == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						int databaseVersion = val.DatabaseVersion;
						int version = val.Version;
						if (version < 22)
						{
							continue;
						}
						int num2 = version.CompareTo(41);
						if (num2 > 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (GlobalControl.bFIPS && version < 34)
						{
							num++;
							string text2 = ApedApplication.APDBUpgrade(val.Alias);
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
							{
								if (text2.Contains("APLDB"))
								{
									arrayList4.Add(text2);
									continue;
								}
								arrayList.Add(val.Alias);
								if (!arrayList2.Contains(text2))
								{
									arrayList2.Add(text2);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								arrayList3.Add(val.Alias);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							AddDatabase(val, userName, userPassword);
						}
					}
					if (num == 1)
					{
						if (arrayList3.Count == 1)
						{
							text = arrayList3[0].ToString() + " was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. You can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
						}
						else if (arrayList.Count == 1)
						{
							string text3 = arrayList2[0].ToString();
							text = arrayList[0].ToString() + " was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. Please use " + text3 + " from the current release instead.";
						}
						else if (arrayList4.Count == 1)
						{
							text = "This version of APLDB was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. Please use the version of APLDB that is FIPS-compliant. See the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
						}
					}
					else if (num > 1)
					{
						int num3 = 0;
						int num4 = 0;
						string text4 = string.Empty;
						string text5 = string.Empty;
						string text6 = string.Empty;
						string text7 = string.Empty;
						for (int j = 0; j < arrayList.Count; j++)
						{
							num3++;
							string text8 = arrayList[j].ToString();
							text4 = ((num3 <= 1) ? (text4 + text8) : (text4 + "," + text8));
						}
						num3 = 0;
						for (int k = 0; k < arrayList2.Count; k++)
						{
							num3++;
							string text9 = arrayList2[k].ToString();
							text5 = ((num3 <= 1) ? (text5 + text9) : (text5 + "," + text9));
						}
						for (int l = 0; l < arrayList3.Count; l++)
						{
							num4++;
							string text10 = arrayList3[l].ToString();
							text6 = ((num4 <= 1) ? (text6 + text10) : (text6 + "," + text10));
						}
						if (arrayList4.Count == 1)
						{
							text7 = arrayList4[0].ToString();
						}
						string UserDBS = string.Empty;
						string APDBS = string.Empty;
						string APNewDBS = string.Empty;
						string text11 = ApedApplication.CreateDBString(text6, text4, text5, text7, out UserDBS, out APDBS, out APNewDBS);
						text = "The databases ";
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(UserDBS))
						{
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
							{
								text = text + APDBS + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled.";
								text = text + " Please use " + APNewDBS + " from the current release instead.";
							}
							else
							{
								text = text + text11 + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. \n \n";
								text = text + "For " + text7 + ", please use the version that is FIPS-compliant. See the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\". \n \n";
								text = text + "For " + APDBS + ", please use " + APNewDBS + " from the current release instead.";
							}
						}
						else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(APDBS))
						{
							if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
							{
								text = text + UserDBS + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled.";
								text += " You can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
							}
							else
							{
								text = text + text11 + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. \n \n";
								text = text + "For " + text7 + ", please use the version that is FIPS-compliant. See the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\". \n \n";
								text = text + "For " + UserDBS + ", you can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
							}
						}
						else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
						{
							text = text + text11 + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. \n \n";
							text = text + "For " + APDBS + ", please use " + APNewDBS + " from the current release instead. \n \n";
							text = text + "For " + UserDBS + ", you can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
						}
						else
						{
							text = text + text11 + " were created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms and cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. \n \n";
							text = text + "For " + text7 + ", please use the version that is FIPS-compliant. See the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\". \n \n";
							text = text + "For " + APDBS + ", please use " + APNewDBS + " from the current release instead. \n \n";
							text = text + "For " + UserDBS + ", you can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
						}
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("An error occurred registering the database: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				finally
				{
					registeredDatabaseForm.Cursor = Cursors.Default;
				}
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void onUpdateDatabaseCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			DialogResult dialogResult = MessageBox.Show("This will update Aspen Properties system databases to the latest delivered version. \nThe existing system databases will be deleted and latest ones are \nrestored. You will need to register any user databases manually. \nClick OK to continue.", "Update Aspen Properties system databases", MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1);
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				DeleteCurrentDatabases();
				RestoreNewDatabases();
				UpdateDatabasesWithConfigFile();
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void DeleteCurrentDatabases()
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0076: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			if (myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase value = item;
					arrayList.Add(value);
				}
				foreach (IDatabase item2 in arrayList)
				{
					IDatabase val = item2;
					if (myDatabaseNodeCollection.ContainsKey(val.Alias))
					{
						DatabaseNode databaseNode = (DatabaseNode)myDatabaseNodeCollection[val.Alias];
						databaseNode.RemoveThisNode();
						if (val.DataSource.Contains("np:") && val.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && val.DataSource.Contains("tsql"))
						{
							databaseNode.DeleteDatabase(val);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			string ConfigurationFilePath = string.Empty;
			ApedApplication.GetConfigurationFilePath(out ConfigurationFilePath);
			if (File.Exists(ConfigurationFilePath))
			{
				string text = ApedApplication.CanTheFileBeDeleted(ConfigurationFilePath);
				text = Path.GetDirectoryName(ConfigurationFilePath);
				text = ApedApplication.CanTheDirectoryBeDeleted(text);
			}
		}

		private void DeleteCurrentDatabases(string configfilepath)
		{
			//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0079: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0080: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string empty = string.Empty;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			if (myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase value = item;
					arrayList.Add(value);
				}
				foreach (IDatabase item2 in arrayList)
				{
					IDatabase val = item2;
					if (myDatabaseNodeCollection.ContainsKey(val.Alias))
					{
						DatabaseNode databaseNode = (DatabaseNode)myDatabaseNodeCollection[val.Alias];
						empty = databaseNode.DropDBWithLocalDB();
					}
				}
			}
			if (File.Exists(configfilepath))
			{
				empty = ApedApplication.CanTheFileBeDeleted(configfilepath);
				empty = Path.GetDirectoryName(configfilepath);
				empty = ApedApplication.CanTheDirectoryBeDeleted(empty);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDatabasesWithConfigFile()
		{
			InitializeDatabasesFromDefaultConfig();
			UpdateAllDatabases();
			if (myDatabases != null && ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count > 0)
			{
				GlobalControl.localDBUpdate = true;
				updateChildNodes();
			}
		}

		private void RestoreNewDatabases()
		{
			Thread thread = new Thread(RunUpdateDatabases);
			thread.IsBackground = true;
			thread.Start();
			_waitForDialog = new WaitForDialog();
			_waitForDialog.title = "Aspen Properties Database Configuration";
			_waitForDialog.label11 = "Please wait while Aspen Properties system databases are being updated.";
			_waitForDialog.label22 = "This is a one-time process and may take a few minutes.";
			_waitForDialog.label33 = "Please stay with this window until the process is complete.";
			_waitForDialog.ShowDialog();
		}

		public void RunUpdateDatabases()
		{
			ApedApplication.RestoreSystemDatabases();
			_waitForDialog.bClose = true;
		}

		private void onRestoreDatabaseCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			if (GlobalControl.Next == 2)
			{
				TestRestoreDatabase();
				return;
			}
			if (GlobalControl.Next == 3)
			{
				UpdateLogin();
				return;
			}
			if (GlobalControl.Next == 4)
			{
				UpdateLogin(GlobalControl.serverName, GlobalControl.userName, GlobalControl.password);
				return;
			}
			LaunchRestoreDatabaseWizard();
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void onConfigureSQLCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			LaunchConfigureSQLManagerDlg();
		}

		private void onSwitchToSQLCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0059: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string ConfigurationFilePath = string.Empty;
			ApedApplication.GetConfigurationFilePath(out ConfigurationFilePath);
			ApedApplication.GetAllBackupFiles();
			ApRestoreDB val = new ApRestoreDB();
			val.SetSwitch();
			((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			if (val.IsCancel())
			{
				return;
			}
			string sqlInstanceName = val.sqlInstanceName;
			string sqlCMDPath = val.sqlCMDPath;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sqlInstanceName) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(sqlCMDPath))
			{
				return;
			}
			IConfigFileInstaller val2 = (IConfigFileInstaller)new CConfigFileInstaller();
			val2.Switch();
			Cursor.Current = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			int num = val2.RestoreDatabasesByDBManager(sqlInstanceName, sqlCMDPath);
			Cursor.Current = Cursors.Default;
			if (num == 0)
			{
				DeleteCurrentDatabases(ConfigurationFilePath);
				GlobalControl.localDBServer = false;
				GlobalControl.sharedFilePath = Path.GetDirectoryName(GlobalControl.defaultConfigurationFilePath);
				myDatabases = (IDatabaseCollection)(object)val2.GetDatabaseCollection();
				updateChildNodes();
				ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
				return;
			}
			GlobalControl.localDBServer = true;
			GlobalControl.Switch = false;
			if (num >= 3)
			{
				if (num == 5)
				{
					string text = "No backup files are available for restoring.";
					MessageBox.Show(text, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
				if (num == 10)
				{
					string text2 = "You do not have permission to restore database.";
					MessageBox.Show(text2, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
				if (num == 15)
				{
					if (GlobalControl.isWinPC)
					{
						string text3 = "You cannot restore databases because you do not have SqlLocalDB or SQL server installed.";
						MessageBox.Show(text3, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
					}
					else
					{
						string text4 = "You cannot restore databases because you do not have SQL server installed.";
						MessageBox.Show(text4, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
					}
				}
				if (num == 22 && GlobalControl.AspenDB == 2)
				{
					string text5 = "You set the registry to access Aspen Properties Enterprise Database via a remote SQL server. Therefore, no databases can be restored to your local machine. Please launch Database Manager to register databases from the remote SQL server.";
					MessageBox.Show(text5, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				string text6 = string.Empty;
				switch (num)
				{
				case 1:
					text6 = ((!GlobalControl.isWinPC) ? "You must have administrative privilege to restore Aspen Properties databases." : "You must run this as administrator to restore Aspen Properties databases.");
					break;
				case 2:
					text6 = "No SQL server instance existed. Install Miscrosoft SQL Server 2012 Express SP4 or later first.";
					break;
				}
				MessageBox.Show(text6, "Database Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
			}
		}

		}

		}

		private void UpdateLogin(string serverName, string login, string password)
		{
			if (serverName.Contains("pipe") && serverName.Contains("tsql") && serverName.Contains("query"))
			{
				APEDSMOGlobal.UpdateLogin(serverName, login, password);
				if (GlobalControl.Next == 4)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("Update login completed.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateLogin()
		{
			if (!GlobalControl.serverName.Contains("pipe") && !GlobalControl.serverName.Contains("tsql") && !GlobalControl.serverName.Contains("query"))
			{
				APEDSMOGlobal.UpdateLogin(GlobalControl.serverName, GlobalControl.userName, GlobalControl.password, GlobalControl.sysDatabases);
				MessageBox.Show("Update login completed.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		private void TestRestoreDatabase()
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			CConfigFileInstaller val = new CConfigFileInstaller();
			string sQLInstanceFromDataSource = ApedApplication.GetSQLInstanceFromDataSource(GlobalControl.serverName);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sQLInstanceFromDataSource) && val.CheckAPEDSecurityLogin(Environment.MachineName, sQLInstanceFromDataSource, GlobalControl.userName, GlobalControl.password) && val.RestoreDatabases(GlobalControl.sysDatabases, Environment.MachineName, sQLInstanceFromDataSource, GlobalControl.userName, GlobalControl.password) && val.InstallConfigurationFile(GlobalControl.sysDatabases, sQLInstanceFromDataSource, GlobalControl.userName, Environment.MachineName, sQLInstanceFromDataSource))
			{
				MessageBox.Show("Restore databases completed.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		}

		private void onExportConfigurationCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			saveFileDialog1 = new SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog1.DefaultExt = "aem";
			saveFileDialog1.Filter = "APED Configuration Files (*.aem)|*.aem";
			saveFileDialog1.InitialDirectory = "C:\\";
			if (saveFileDialog1.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				try
				{
					Stream stream = new FileStream(saveFileDialog1.FileName, FileMode.Create);
					myDatabases.ExportConfigurationFile(stream);
					stream.Close();
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("An error occured while exporting the configuration file: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void onDatabaseSynchronizationCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0081: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0088: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0101: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0108: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0195: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_019c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_019d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01a4: Expected O, but got Unknown
			try
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				IDatabase val = null;
				IDatabase targetDatabase = null;
				int num = 0;
				foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase val2 = item;
					if (val2.Alias == node.DisplayName)
					{
						arrayList.Add(val2.Alias);
					}
				}
				foreach (IDatabase item2 in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase val3 = item2;
					if (val3.Alias != node.DisplayName)
					{
						arrayList.Add(val3.Alias);
					}
				}
				SynchronizationSelectionForm synchronizationSelectionForm = new SynchronizationSelectionForm();
				synchronizationSelectionForm.Databases = arrayList;
				if (synchronizationSelectionForm.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
				{
					return;
				}
				num = synchronizationSelectionForm.SynchronizationType;
				foreach (IDatabase item3 in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase val4 = item3;
					if (val4.Alias == synchronizationSelectionForm.SourceDatabase)
					{
						val = val4;
					}
					if (val4.Alias == synchronizationSelectionForm.TargetDatabase)
					{
						targetDatabase = val4;
					}
				}
				if (targetDatabase == null)
				{
					targetDatabase = synchronizationSelectionForm.AddedDatabase;
				}
				if (num == 3)
				{
					CreateNewDatabase1(out targetDatabase);
				}
				else if (!CheckDatabaseTypes(val, targetDatabase))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (SyncChecks(val, targetDatabase))
				{
					IDatabaseSynchronizationSource source = (IDatabaseSynchronizationSource)val;
					IDatabaseSynchronizationTarget target = (IDatabaseSynchronizationTarget)targetDatabase;
					switch (num)
					{
					case 1:
						CaseOne(val, targetDatabase, source, target);
						break;
					case 2:
						CaseTwo(val, targetDatabase, source, target);
						break;
					case 3:
						CaseThree(val, targetDatabase, source, target);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred while synchronizing the databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			if (mybSLMCheck)
			{
				return;
			}
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			mybExpand = true;
			try
			{
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode2 = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode2).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			if (!mybSLMCheck)
			{
				((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
				try
				{
					ShowGeneral("");
					return true;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the database information: " + ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				finally
				{
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_008f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0091: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00c5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cc: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowGeneral(database);
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType != 8 || myApedDatabases.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			myDatabases.Clear();
			foreach (IApedDatabase value in myApedDatabases.Values)
			{
				IApedDatabase val = value;
				IDatabase database2 = val.Database;
				myDatabases.Add(database2);
			}
			updateChildNodes();
		}

		private void updateChildNodes()
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
			foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
			{
				IDatabase database = item;
				addDatabaseNode(database);
			}
		}

		private void ShowGeneral(string db)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0041: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedDatabasesCtl val = (ApedDatabasesCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (myApedDatabases.Count == 0 && myDatabases != null)
			{
				foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase val2 = item;
					AddApedDatabase(val2, val2.UserNameByReg, val2.PasswordByReg, out var apedDatabase);
					if (apedDatabase != null && !myApedDatabases.Contains(apedDatabase.Name))
					{
						myApedDatabases.Add(apedDatabase.Name, apedDatabase);
					}
				}
			}
			if (myApedDatabases.Count > 0)
			{
				val.IsReadOnly = true;
				val.SetApedDatabases(myApedDatabases, db);
			}
			((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
		}

		}

			((BaseNode)this).OnRefresh();
		}

		public void AddDatabase(IDatabase database)
		{
			database.ConfirmApplicationPwd("ATVIEWER");
			int num = myDatabases.IndexOf(database.Alias);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				myDatabases.RemoveAt(num);
			}
			else
			{
				num = ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count;
			}
			myDatabases.Insert(num, database);
			if (mybExpand)
			{
				addDatabaseNode(database);
			}
		}

		public void AddDatabase(IDatabase database, string userName, string userPassword)
		{
			database.ConfirmApplicationPwd("ATVIEWER");
			int num = myDatabases.IndexOf(database.Alias);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				myDatabases.RemoveAt(num);
			}
			else
			{
				num = ((ICollection)myDatabases).Count;
			}
			myDatabases.Insert(num, database);
			if (mybExpand)
			{
				addDatabaseNode(database, userName, userPassword);
			}
		}

		private void addDatabaseNode(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown
			AddApedDatabase(database, out var apedDatabase);
			if (apedDatabase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DatabaseNode databaseNode = new DatabaseNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, this, database, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			foreach (BaseNode child in ((BaseNode)this).Children)
			{
				BaseNode val = child;
				if (val.DisplayName == database.Alias)
				{
					val.Remove(false);
				}
			}
			try
			{
				((BaseNode)databaseNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (!myDatabaseNodeCollection.ContainsKey(database.Alias))
			{
				myDatabaseNodeCollection.Add(database.Alias, databaseNode);
			}
		}

		private void addDatabaseNode(IDatabase database, string userName, string userPassword)
		{
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Expected O, but got Unknown
			AddApedDatabase(database, userName, userPassword, out var apedDatabase);
			if (apedDatabase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DatabaseNode databaseNode = new DatabaseNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, this, database, myApedDatabases, myDataManager);
			foreach (BaseNode child in ((BaseNode)this).Children)
			{
				BaseNode val = child;
				if (val.DisplayName == database.Alias)
				{
					val.Remove(false);
				}
			}
			try
			{
				((BaseNode)databaseNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (!myDatabaseNodeCollection.ContainsKey(database.Alias))
			{
				myDatabaseNodeCollection.Add(database.Alias, databaseNode);
			}
		}

		private void AddApedDatabase(IDatabase database, out IApedDatabase apedDatabase1)
		{
			//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0069: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			apedDatabase1 = null;
			string alias = database.Alias;
			if (!myApedDatabases.Contains(alias))
			{
				IApedDatabase val = (IApedDatabase)new ApedDatabase();
				val.Name = alias;
				val.Database = database;
				val.UserName = database.UserNameByReg;
				val.Password = database.PasswordByReg;
				myApedDatabases.Add(alias, val);
				apedDatabase1 = val;
			}
			else
			{
				apedDatabase1 = (IApedDatabase)myApedDatabases[alias];
			}
			if (database.DataManagerAccess)
			{
				apedDatabase1.bView = true;
			}
			else
			{
				apedDatabase1.bView = false;
			}
		}

		private void AddApedDatabase(IDatabase database, string userName, string userPassword, out IApedDatabase apedDatabase1)
		{
			//IL_00a0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a6: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			apedDatabase1 = null;
			string alias = database.Alias;
			if (!myApedDatabases.Contains(alias))
			{
				IApedDatabase val = (IApedDatabase)new ApedDatabase();
				val.Name = alias;
				val.Database = database;
				val.UserName = userName;
				val.Password = userPassword;
				if (database.DataSource.Contains("np:") && database.DataSource.Contains("pipe") && database.DataSource.Contains("tsql") && !GlobalControl.bLocalDBSQL)
				{
					val.bLocalDBSQL = false;
				}
				myApedDatabases.Add(alias, val);
				apedDatabase1 = val;
			}
			else
			{
				apedDatabase1 = (IApedDatabase)myApedDatabases[alias];
			}
			if (database.DataManagerAccess)
			{
				apedDatabase1.bView = true;
			}
			else
			{
				apedDatabase1.bView = false;
			}
		}

		private void CreateNewDatabase1(out IDatabase targetDatabase)
		{
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0048: Expected O, but got Unknown
			targetDatabase = null;
			RegisteredDatabaseForm registeredDatabaseForm = new RegisteredDatabaseForm();
			if (registeredDatabaseForm.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			if (registeredDatabaseForm.Username != "")
			{
				text = registeredDatabaseForm.Username;
				text2 = registeredDatabaseForm.Password;
			}
			try
			{
				if (registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseType == RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes.SqlServer)
				{
					SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
					string licMgrString = ((IDatabase)val).GetLicMgrString();
					string text3 = "";
					string text4 = "CREATORPWD";
					val.CreateNew(registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias[0].ToString(), registeredDatabaseForm.ServerName, text, text2, registeredDatabaseForm.InitialCatalog[0].ToString(), licMgrString, text3, text4);
					targetDatabase = (IDatabase)(object)val;
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show("This function is not supported for Oracle databases", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred creating the database: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		private bool CheckDatabaseTypes(IDatabase sourceDatabase, IDatabase targetDatabase)
		{
			if (targetDatabase is SqlServerDatabase && sourceDatabase is SqlServerDatabase)
			{
				return true;
			}
			MessageBox.Show("Both the source and target database must be the same type, either SQL or Oracle.  Please change your database selections.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
			return false;
		}

		private bool SyncChecks(IDatabase sourceDatabase, IDatabase targetDatabase)
		{
			if (sourceDatabase == null)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("The source database is not defined.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				return false;
			}
			if (targetDatabase == null)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("The target database is not defined.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				return false;
			}
			if (targetDatabase == sourceDatabase)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("The target database and source database are the same.  Please change your database selections.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void CaseOne(IDatabase sourceDatabase, IDatabase targetDatabase, IDatabaseSynchronizationSource source, IDatabaseSynchronizationTarget target)
		{
			if (target.DatabaseIdentity == source.DatabaseIdentity)
			{
				string text = "Do you want to flush the contents of the target database before synchronizing?  If you flush the target database, you will not be able to restore the existing data in " + targetDatabase.Alias + ".";
				if (MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.No)
				{
					if (ConfirmSynchro())
					{
						((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
						try
						{
							target.SynchronizeTo(source, false);
							AddDatabase(targetDatabase);
						}
						finally
						{
							((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
						}
						MessageBox.Show("The databases were successfully synchronized.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
					}
				}
				else if (ConfirmSynchro())
				{
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
					try
					{
						target.SynchronizeTo(source, true);
						AddDatabase(targetDatabase);
					}
					finally
					{
						((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
					}
					MessageBox.Show("The databases were successfully synchronized.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
				return;
			}
			string text2 = "The database identities are different.  In order to synchronize " + sourceDatabase.Alias + " to " + targetDatabase.Alias + ", the existing data in the target database must be flushed of data.  By doing this, you will not be able to restore the existing data in the target database.  Do you wish to continue?";
			if (MessageBox.Show(text2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question) != DialogResult.No && ConfirmSynchro())
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				try
				{
					target.SynchronizeTo(source, true);
					AddDatabase(targetDatabase);
				}
				finally
				{
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
				}
				MessageBox.Show("The databases were successfully synchronized.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
			}
		}

		private void CaseTwo(IDatabase sourceDatabase, IDatabase targetDatabase, IDatabaseSynchronizationSource source, IDatabaseSynchronizationTarget target)
		{
			try
			{
				if (target.UtcTimeOfLastSyncronization.Ticks == 0L)
				{
					MessageBox.Show("You can not copy the database changes until a full synchronization has occured.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
				else if (ConfirmSynchro())
				{
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
					source.ApplyChangesTo(target);
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
					MessageBox.Show("The databases were successfully synchronized.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		private void CaseThree(IDatabase sourceDatabase, IDatabase targetDatabase, IDatabaseSynchronizationSource source, IDatabaseSynchronizationTarget target)
		{
			try
			{
				if (ConfirmSynchro())
				{
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
					target.SynchronizeTo(source, true);
					((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
					MessageBox.Show("The databases were successfully synchronized.", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		private bool ConfirmSynchro()
		{
			if (MessageBox.Show("Synchronizing this database may take several minutes.  Do you wish to continue?", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question) == DialogResult.Yes)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool DoesDatabaseNameExist(ArrayList dbNames)
		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (string dbName in dbNames)
			{
				foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)myDatabases)
				{
					IDatabase val = item;
					string alias = val.Alias;
					if (dbName.ToUpperInvariant() == alias.ToUpperInvariant())
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private string GetFilePathForImport()
		{
			openFileDialog1 = new OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog1.DefaultExt = "aem";
			openFileDialog1.Filter = "APED Config Files (*.aem)|*.aem";
			openFileDialog1.InitialDirectory = "C:\\";
			DialogResult dialogResult = openFileDialog1.ShowDialog();
			if (dialogResult == DialogResult.OK)
			{
				return openFileDialog1.FileName;
			}
			return "";
		}

		private bool DatabaseNameExists(string checkAlias)
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			foreach (BaseNode childNode in ((BaseNode)this).m_ChildNodes)
			{
				BaseNode val = childNode;
				if (val.DisplayName == checkAlias)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool RemoveDatabaseFromSelection(IDatabase db)
		{
			if (myDatabases != null && myDatabases.Contains(db.Alias))
			{
				myDatabases.Remove(db);
				if (myApedDatabases.ContainsKey(db.Alias))
				{
					myApedDatabases.Remove(db.Alias);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void RestoreDatabaseFromSelection(IDatabase db)
		{
			AddDatabase(db);
		}

		private bool InitializeDatabasesFromDefaultConfig()
		{
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0063: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!GlobalControl.localDBOwner)
			{
				CConfigFileInstaller val = new CConfigFileInstaller();
				val.CreateConfigurationFile();
			}
			myDatabases = (IDatabaseCollection)new DatabaseCollection();
			myDatabases.DatabaseAdded += new DatabaseCollectionEventHandlerDelegate(DatabaseCollectionChange);
			myDatabases.DatabaseChanged += new DatabaseCollectionEventHandlerDelegate(DatabaseCollectionChange);
			myDatabases.DatabaseRemoved += new DatabaseCollectionEventHandlerDelegate(DatabaseCollectionChange);
			try
			{
				string text = "ATVIEWER";
				myDatabases.InitializeFromConfigurationFile((Stream)null, text);
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool InitializeDatabasesFromAnyConfig()
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0062: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string filePathForImport = GetFilePathForImport();
			if (filePathForImport == "")
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			IDatabaseCollection val = (IDatabaseCollection)new DatabaseCollection();
			Stream stream = new FileStream(filePathForImport, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.Read);
			using (stream)
			{
				string text = "ATVIEWER";
				val.InitializeFromConfigurationFile(stream, text);
			}
			foreach (IDatabase item in (IEnumerable)val)
			{
				IDatabase val2 = item;
				if (!myDatabases.Contains(val2.Alias))
				{
					myDatabases.Add(val2);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void DatabaseCollectionChange(IDatabase database)
		{
			myDatabases.ExportConfigurationFile((Stream)null);
		}

		internal void LaunchManageDatabaseWizard()
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedGlobal.bAbort = false;
			myDatabasesWizard = null;
			ApedDatabasesWizardForm val = new ApedDatabasesWizardForm(myApedDatabases);
			val.FinishClicked += new ApedClickEventHandler(OnFinishClicked);
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			if (ApedGlobal.bAbort || myDatabasesWizard == null || myDatabasesWizard.ApedDatabase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IApedDatabase apedDatabase = myDatabasesWizard.ApedDatabase;
			if (!apedDatabase.bProperty)
			{
				string logFileName = val.LogFileName;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(logFileName))
				{
					FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(logFileName);
					string fullName = fileInfo.FullName;
					string directoryName = fileInfo.DirectoryName;
					if (!Directory.Exists(directoryName))
					{
						Directory.CreateDirectory(directoryName);
					}
					ApedUtils.CreatePropertiesAndUnits(apedDatabase, val.LogFileName);
				}
			}
			IDatabase database = myDatabasesWizard.Database;
			if (database != null)
			{
				if (apedDatabase.DataVersion.CompareTo(database.DataVersion) != 0)
				{
					database.UpdateDatabaseVersion(apedDatabase.DataVersion);
					myDatabasesWizard.Database = database;
				}
				AddDatabase(myDatabasesWizard.Database, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
				apedDatabase.bSetup = false;
				if (val.bCompoundsChanged)
				{
					apedDatabase.bNewCompounds = true;
				}
				if (myApedDatabases.ContainsKey(myDatabasesWizard.Database.Alias))
				{
					myApedDatabases[apedDatabase.Name] = apedDatabase;
				}
				else
				{
					myApedDatabases.Add(apedDatabase.Name, apedDatabase);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnFinishClicked(object sender, object changedPropertyList)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabasesWizard = (IApedDatabaseObject)sender;
			IApedDatabase apedDatabase = myDatabasesWizard.ApedDatabase;
			IDatabase database = null;
			string name = apedDatabase.Name;
			string text = name;
			string userName = apedDatabase.UserName;
			string password = apedDatabase.Password;
			string serverType = apedDatabase.ServerType;
			string serverName = apedDatabase.ServerName;
			string creatorPwd = apedDatabase.CreatorPwd;
			string dataVersion = apedDatabase.DataVersion;
			if (!GlobalControl.localDBServer)
			{
				string text2 = GlobalControl.sharedFilePath + "\\" + name + ".mdf";
				if (File.Exists(text2))
				{
					text2 = "Failed to create the new database: Cannot create file '" + text2 + "' because it already exists.";
					MessageBox.Show(text2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					return;
				}
			}
			CreateNewDatabase(serverType, serverName, name, userName, password, creatorPwd, out database);
			if (database != null)
			{
				database.UpdateDatabaseVersion(dataVersion);
				myDatabasesWizard.Database = database;
			}
		}

		internal void LaunchDownloadDatabaseWizard()
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedGlobal.bAbort = false;
			myDownloadDatabase = null;
			ApedDownloadDatabaseForm val = new ApedDownloadDatabaseForm(myApedDatabases);
			val.OKClicked += new ApedClickEventHandler(OnOKClicked);
			string databaseType = "SQL Server";
			GetLocalServerNames(databaseType, out var localServerNames);
			if (localServerNames != null)
			{
				val.LocalServerNames = localServerNames;
			}
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			if (ApedGlobal.bAbort)
			{
				return;
			}
			IApedDatabase apedDatabase = myDownloadDatabase.ApedDatabase;
			if (apedDatabase != null)
			{
				AddDatabase(myDownloadDatabase.Database);
				apedDatabase.bSetup = false;
				if (myApedDatabases.ContainsKey(myDatabasesWizard.Database.Alias))
				{
					myApedDatabases[apedDatabase.Name] = apedDatabase;
				}
				else
				{
					myApedDatabases.Add(apedDatabase.Name, apedDatabase);
				}
			}
		}

			CreateNewDatabase(serverType, serverName, name, userName, password, creatorPwd, out database);
			if (database != null)

				myDownloadDatabase.Database = database;
			}
		}

		internal void LaunchRestoreDatabaseWizard()
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0033: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00e5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ec: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedGlobal.bAbort = false;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			string empty3 = string.Empty;
			myRestoreDatabaseWizard = null;
			ApedRestoreDatabaseForm val = new ApedRestoreDatabaseForm(myApedDatabases);
			val.FinishClicked += new ApedClickEventHandler(OnFinishRestoreClicked);
			DialogResult dialogResult = ((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			if (ApedGlobal.bAbort)
			{
				return;
			}
			IApedDatabase apedDatabase = val.ApedDatabase;
			empty3 = val.RestoreFileName;
			if (apedDatabase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (GlobalControl.bSMO)
			{
				flag = ((!apedDatabase.ServerName.Contains("pipe") || !apedDatabase.ServerName.Contains("tsql") || !apedDatabase.ServerName.Contains("query")) ? APEDSMOGlobal.LinkingToSQLServer(apedDatabase.ServerName, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password) : APEDSMOGlobal.LinkingToSQLServer(apedDatabase.ServerName, "", ""));
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				SqlServerDatabase val2 = new SqlServerDatabase();
				try
				{
					if (!val2.Connect(apedDatabase.Name, apedDatabase.ServerName, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password, apedDatabase.Name))
					{
						return;
					}
					IDatabase val3 = (IDatabase)(object)val2;
					if (GlobalControl.bSMO && !ApedApplication.IsLocalDB(apedDatabase.ServerName))
					{
						empty = APEDSMOGlobal.AddUsertoDatabase(apedDatabase.ServerName, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password, apedDatabase.Name, "db_datareader");
					}
					string dataVersion = val3.DataVersion;
					int databaseVersion = val3.DatabaseVersion;
					int version = val3.Version;
					if (GlobalControl.bFIPS && version < 34)
					{
						string text = ApedApplication.APDBUpgrade(val3.Alias);
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							string alias = val3.Alias;
							empty2 = ((!text.Contains("APLDB")) ? ("The database backup file for " + val3.Alias.ToString() + " was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms. It can be restored but cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. Please use " + text + " from the current release instead.") : ("The database backup file for " + val3.Alias.ToString() + " was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms. It can be restored but cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. Please use the version of APLDB that is FIPS-compliant. See the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\"."));
						}
						else
						{
							empty2 = "The database backup file for " + val3.Alias.ToString() + " was created with non-FIPS validated cryptographic algorithms. It can be restored but cannot be registered when FIPS-compliant is enabled. You can either disable FIPS-compliant or re-create the database. Please see Database Manager Help for FIPS-compliant and how to create user database.";
						}
						MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						DeleteDatabase1(val3);
						return;
					}
					string fileName = Path.GetFileName(empty3);
					if (fileName.Contains("APLDB"))
					{
						if (GlobalControl.bFIPS)
						{
							if (version == 34 && (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB88") == 0))
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								return;
							}
							if (version == 38 && (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB120") == 0))
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								return;
							}
							if (version == 41 && (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB150") == 0))
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								return;
							}
							empty2 = string.Empty;
							if (version == 34)
							{
								empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V8.8 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB88. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							}
							if (version == 38)
							{
								empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V12 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB120. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							}
							if (version == 41)
							{
								empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V15 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB150. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							}
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
							{
								MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
								DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							}
							return;
						}
						switch (version)
						{
						case 27:
							if (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0)
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								break;
							}
							empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V7.2 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							break;
						case 32:
							if (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB84") == 0)
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								break;
							}
							empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V8.4 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB84. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							break;
						case 34:
							if (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB88") == 0)
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								break;
							}
							empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V8.8 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB88. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							break;
						case 38:
							if (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB120") == 0)
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								break;
							}
							empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V12 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB120. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							break;
						case 41:
							if (apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB") == 0 || apedDatabase.Name.CompareTo("APLDB150") == 0)
							{
								AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
								break;
							}
							empty2 = "The database backup file APLDB from V15 can be restored but cannot be registered as " + apedDatabase.Name + ". It must be restored to a new database as APLDB or APLDB150. Please see the online help topic: \"Maintaining Access to Retired Databanks\".";
							MessageBox.Show(empty2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
							DeleteDatabase1(val3);
							break;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						AddDatabase(val3, apedDatabase.UserName, apedDatabase.Password);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(apedDatabase.UserName))
					{
						empty = "Failed to restore the database with the server " + apedDatabase.ServerName + ". You do not have the permission using Windows authentication.";
						MessageBox.Show(empty, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			string text2 = "Linking to the server, " + apedDatabase.ServerName + ", failed.";
			MessageBox.Show(text2, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
		}

		public void DeleteDatabase1(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).Delete();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				string text = "Error occurred while trying to delete Database " + database.Alias + " permanently from this server. Make sure you have permission to drop databases from this server.";
				MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		private void OnFinishRestoreClicked(object sender, object changedPropertyList)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myRestoreDatabaseWizard = (IApedDatabaseObject)sender;

			string text = name;
			string userName = apedDatabase.UserName;
			string password = apedDatabase.Password;

			string serverName = apedDatabase.ServerName;

			CreateNewDatabase(serverType, serverName, name, userName, password, creatorPwd, out database);
			if (database != null)
			{
				myRestoreDatabaseWizard.Database = database;
			}
		}

		public void CreateNewDatabase(string databaseType, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string creator_Pwd, out IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			database = null;
			try
			{
				if (databaseType.CompareTo("SQL Server") == 0)
				{
					SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
					string text = "";
					if (creator_Pwd != null && creator_Pwd != "")
					{
						text = ((IDatabase)val).GetLicMgrString();
					}
					string text2 = "";
					val.CreateNew(databaseName, serverName, userName, userPassword, databaseName, text, text2, creator_Pwd);
					database = (IDatabase)(object)val;
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show("This function is not supported for Oracle databases", "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				string value = databaseName + ".mdf";
				string text3 = "because it already exists";
				if (ex.Message.Contains(value) && ex.Message.Contains(text3))
				{
					int num = ex.Message.IndexOf(text3);
					value = ex.Message.Substring(0, num + text3.Length + 1);
					MessageBox.Show(value, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(userName))
				{
					string text4 = "Failed to create the new database with the server " + serverName + ". You do not have the permission using Windows authentication.";
					MessageBox.Show(text4, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred creating the database: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		public void RegisterDatabase(string databaseType, string databaseAlias, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, out IDatabase database)
		{
			database = null;
		}

		public void DeleteDatabase(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (DeleteDBWithSQLMode(database))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).Delete();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string text = "Error occurred while trying to delete Database " + database.Alias + " permanently from this server.\n" + ex.Message;
				MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
		}

		public bool DeleteDBWithSQLMode(IDatabase database)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			bool flag = true;
			try
			{
				((SqlServerDatabase)database).Delete1();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string text = "Error occurred while trying to delete Database " + database.Alias + " permanently from this server.\n" + ex.Message;
				flag = false;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public bool BackupDatabase(DatabaseProviderType databaseType, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string backupToFileName)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public bool RestoreDatabase(DatabaseProviderType databaseType, string serverName, string databaseName, string userName, string userPassword, string restoreFromFileName)

		}

		private string ConfigFileCheck(out string configfilepath)
		{
			string text = "";
			configfilepath = "";
			ApedApplication.GetConfigurationFilePath(out configfilepath);
			if (!File.Exists(configfilepath))
			{
				return "";
			}
			text = ApedApplication.IsConfigureFileWritable(configfilepath);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				ApedApplication.ReadConfigFile(configfilepath);
				if (GlobalControl.listOfRegisteredDatabases != null && GlobalControl.listOfRegisteredDatabases.Count > 0)
				{
					text = "OK";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text = " file is not writable. You will not be able to register/unregister/read existing databases or create new databases.\n";
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void OnGetIPAddressCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(GlobalControl.ipAddress))
			{
				MessageBox.Show("IP Address: " + GlobalControl.ipAddress, "IP Address", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
			}
			else
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred while getting IP Address.", "IP Address", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.None);
			}
		}

		public string GetBDProvider(string regKey, string regValue)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				RegistryKey registryKey2 = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Registry64);
				if (registryKey2 != null)
				{
					registryKey = registryKey2.OpenSubKey(regKey);
					if (registryKey != null)
					{
						object value = registryKey.GetValue(regValue);
						if (value != null)
						{
							text = Convert.ToString(value);
						}
						registryKey.Close();
					}
					registryKey2.Close();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				empty = ex.Message;
			}
			return text;
		}
	}
	internal delegate void DbankCompoundListEventHandler(DbankCompoundList sender, IDbankCompound compound);
	internal delegate void DbankCompoundsEventHandler(DbankCompoundList sender, ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName);
	internal delegate void DatabaseUpdatedEventHandler(object sender, string databaseName);
	internal class DbankCompoundList : IEnumerable
	{
		private ArrayList myListImpl;

		public int Count => myListImpl.Count;

		public event DbankCompoundListEventHandler CompoundAdded;

		public event DbankCompoundListEventHandler CompoundRemoved;

		public event DbankCompoundsEventHandler CompoundsAdded;

		public event DbankCompoundsEventHandler CompoundsRemoved;

		public event DatabaseUpdatedEventHandler DatabaseUpdated;

		public DbankCompoundList()
		{
			myListImpl = new ArrayList();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return myListImpl.GetEnumerator();
		}

		public void Add(IDbankCompound dbankCompound)
		{
			if (dbankCompound == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("dbankCompound");
			}
			if (!ContainsCorresponding(dbankCompound.CompoundObjectOfId))
			{
				myListImpl.Add(dbankCompound);
				OnCompoundAdded(dbankCompound);
			}
		}

		public void Remove(IDbankCompound dbankCompound)
		{
			if (dbankCompound == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("dbankCompound");
			}
			int num = myListImpl.IndexOf(dbankCompound);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				myListImpl.RemoveAt(num);
				OnCompoundRemoved(dbankCompound);
			}
		}

		public void Adds(ArrayList dbankCompounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)
		{
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0025: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (dbankCompounds == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("dbankCompounds");
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (IDbankCompound dbankCompound in dbankCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = dbankCompound;
				if (!ContainsThisName(val.AspenName))
				{
					myListImpl.Add(val);
					if (!flag)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				OnCompoundsAdded(dbankCompounds, nodeType, nodeName);
			}
		}

		public void Removes(ArrayList dbankCompounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)
		{
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0025: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_006a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (dbankCompounds == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("dbankCompounds");
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (IDbankCompound dbankCompound in dbankCompounds)
			{
				IDbankCompound value = dbankCompound;
				int num = myListImpl.IndexOf(value);
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					myListImpl.RemoveAt(num);
					if (!flag)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				OnCompoundsRemoved(dbankCompounds, nodeType, nodeName);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveCorresponding(ICompoundObject compoundObject)
		{
			int num = IndexOfCorresponding(compoundObject);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				object obj = myListImpl[num];
				IDbankCompound compound = (IDbankCompound)((obj is IDbankCompound) ? obj : null);
				myListImpl.RemoveAt(num);
				OnCompoundRemoved(compound);
			}
		}

		public int IndexOfCorresponding(ICompoundObject compoundObject)
		{
			//IL_0030: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (compoundObject == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("compoundObject");
			}
			int result = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < myListImpl.Count; i++)
			{
				object obj = myListImpl[i];
				if (((IDbankCompound)((obj is IDbankCompound) ? obj : null)).Compound.Id == Convert.ToInt32(((CompoundObject)compoundObject).Id))
				{
					result = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool ContainsCorresponding(ICompoundObject compoundObject)
		{
			if (IndexOfCorresponding(compoundObject) >= 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool ContainsThisName(string compoundName)
		{
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myListImpl.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in myListImpl)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					if (compoundName == val.AspenName)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void GetCompound(string compoundName, out IDbankCompound dbCompound)
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			dbCompound = null;
			if (myListImpl.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IDbankCompound item in myListImpl)
			{
				IDbankCompound val = item;
				if (compoundName == val.AspenName)
				{
					dbCompound = val;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			if (myListImpl.Count > 0)
			{
				myListImpl.Clear();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCompoundAdded(IDbankCompound compound)
		{
			if (this.CompoundAdded != null)
			{
				this.CompoundAdded(this, compound);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCompoundRemoved(IDbankCompound compound)
		{
			if (this.CompoundRemoved != null)
			{
				this.CompoundRemoved(this, compound);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCompoundsAdded(ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (this.CompoundsAdded != null)
			{
				this.CompoundsAdded(this, compounds, nodeType, nodeName);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCompoundsRemoved(ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)
		{
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (this.CompoundsRemoved != null)
			{
				this.CompoundsRemoved(this, compounds, nodeType, nodeName);
			}
		}

		public void DatabaseChanged(string databaseName)
		{
			if (databaseName != null && databaseName != "")
			{
				OnDatabaseUpdated(databaseName);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDatabaseUpdated(string databaseName)
		{
			if (this.DatabaseUpdated != null)
			{
				this.DatabaseUpdated(this, databaseName);
			}
		}
	}

		private string myOutputFileName = "";

		private ICompoundManager myCompoundManager;

		private IPropertyManager myPropertyManager;

		public ImportInputFile(IDatabase database)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			myAliasList = new Hashtable();
			openFileDialog1.InitialDirectory = GlobalControl.sharedFilePath;
			saveFileDialog1.InitialDirectory = GlobalControl.sharedFilePath;
			myDatabase = database;
			if (myDatabase != null)
			{
				SetupDatabase();
			}
		}

		private void buttonImport_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (openFileDialog1.FileName != null && openFileDialog1.FileName != "")
			{
				openFileDialog1.FileName = "";
			}
			if (openFileDialog1.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.Cancel)
			{
				myInputFile = openFileDialog1.FileName;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(myInputFile))
				{
					ProcessInputFile();
				}
			}
		}

		private void buttonOK_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void buttonCancel_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void SetupDatabase()
		{
			if (myCompoundManager == null)
			{
				IDatabase obj = myDatabase;
				ISupportsCompounds val = (ISupportsCompounds)(object)((obj is ISupportsCompounds) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					myCompoundManager = val.CompoundManager;
					myCompoundManager.GetIdentifier("ALIAS", ref myAlias);
				}
			}
			if (myPropertyManager == null)
			{
				IDatabase obj2 = myDatabase;
				ISupportsProperties val2 = (ISupportsProperties)(object)((obj2 is ISupportsProperties) ? obj2 : null);
				if (val2 != null)
				{
					myPropertyManager = val2.PropertyManager;
				}
			}
			myDatabase.EnumerateDatabanks(ref myDatabanks);
		}

		private void ProcessInputFile()
		{
			string text = "";
			string[] array = null;
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			int num3 = -1;
			int num4 = -1;
			int num5 = -1;
			int num6 = -1;
			int num7 = -1;
			StreamReader streamReader = File.OpenText(myInputFile);
			while (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text = streamReader.ReadLine()))
			{
				if (text.StartsWith("COMPONENTS"))
				{
					num2 = 0;
				}
				else if (text.Contains("PROP-LIST GMPTPS"))
				{
					num3 = 0;
				}
				else if (text.Contains("PROP-LIST GMPTP1"))
				{
					num4 = 0;
				}
				else if (text.Contains("PROP-LIST GMPTP2"))
				{
					num5 = 0;
				}
				else if (text.Contains("PROP-LIST GMPTP3"))
				{
					num6 = 0;
				}
				else if (text.Contains("PROP-LIST GMPTP4"))
				{
					num7 = 0;
				}
				else if (num3 == -1 && num2 >= 0)
				{
					array = text.Split((string[])null, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
					myAliasList[num2] = array[1];
					num2 = ((!text.EndsWith("/")) ? (num2 + 1) : (-1));
				}
			}
			if (myAliasList.Count > 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < myAliasList.Count; i++)
				{
					string text2 = myAliasList[i].ToString();
					ICompound value = null;
					myCompoundManager.GetCompound(text2, myAlias, ref value);
					myCompoundList[i] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && components != null)
			{
				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void AddCompoundsToSelectedList(DbankCompoundList sender, ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		private bool DBContainCompound(IDbankCompound dbc, ArrayList dbankcompIDs)
		{
			foreach (int dbankcompID in dbankcompIDs)
			{
				string text = dbankcompID.ToString();
				if (text == dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a0: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4

			{

			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0075: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0077: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedPairDataTabCtl val = (ApedPairDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowAllCompounds(val);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedPairDataTabCtl val = (ApedPairDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedPairDataTabCtl pairDataTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				pairDataTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				pairDataTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{

			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

			}
			else

			}
		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName) || !ApedGlobal.bEdit)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedPairTabCtl val = (ApedPairTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;

					{
						ApedGlobal.bEdit = false;

						val.ReleaseExcel();
					}
					((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
