		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedPairTabCtl val = (ApedPairTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 19 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

					{

					}

			}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedPairTabCtl val = (ApedPairTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedPairTabCtl pairTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.bDatabanks)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllDatabanks(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				pairTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				pairTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		{
			return false;
		}

		}

			{
				selectedDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			}
			else
			{
				selectedDbanks = null;
			}
		}

		private void AddHandlers()
		{

		private void AddMenuItems()
		{
			//IL_0032: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_0049: Expected O, but got Unknown

			//IL_006e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_007c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0082: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0093: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres") && (GlobalControl.localDBServer || GlobalControl.runAdmin))
			{
				MenuCommandHandler val = new MenuCommandHandler(OnManagePropertiesAndParametersCommand);
				MenuItem val2 = new MenuItem("&Properties and Parameters...", "Add, View, and Edit Properties and Parameters in Database", val);
				((BaseNode)this).AddTopMenuItem(val2);
				val = new MenuCommandHandler(OnViewChangesCommand);
				val2 = new MenuItem("&View Pending Changes...", "View Changes to Database", val);
				((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);
				val = new MenuCommandHandler(OnSubmitChangesCommand);
				val2 = new MenuItem("&Submit Pending Changes...", "Submit Changes to Database", val);
				((BaseNode)this).AddTaskMenuItem(val2);
			}
		}

		private void onToolbarClickEventHandler(object sender, NodeEventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			int command = ((CommandArgs)args).m_command;
			if (command == 2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("Toolbar AddDatabank16 " + command);
			}
		}

		public override void OnSelectScope()
		{
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0061: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnSelectScope();
			((BaseNode)this).m_Snapin.AddCurrentToolbarsInfo(4);
			if (myApedDatabase.DatabaseRole == 1 && !(myApedDatabase.DBProvider.ToLowerInvariant() == "postgres"))
			{
				return;
			}
			ArrayList menuItems = ((BaseNode)myNode).m_MenuItems;
			if (menuItems == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (MenuItem item in menuItems)
			{
				MenuItem val = item;
				if (val.Name == "&Properties and Parameters..." || val.Name == "&View Pending Changes..." || val.Name == "&Submit Pending Changes...")
				{
					val.Visible = false;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override int GetScopeViewImageIndex(bool isClosed, out bool isGlobal)
		{
			isGlobal = true;
			return 3;
		}

			((BaseNode)this).ClosedImageIndex = 3;
			((BaseNode)this).OpenImageIndex = 3;
			((BaseNode)this).LargeImageIndex = 3;
			((BaseNode)this).SmallImageIndex = 3;
		}

			((BaseNode)this).ResultPaneImages = snapin.Images;
		}

		private void OnManagePropertiesAndParametersCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			LaunchPropertiesAndParametersManagerDlg();
			ApedGlobal.DataManager.Update((ApedUpdateType)1, "");
		}

		private void OnViewChangesCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			LaunchViewChangesForm();
		}

		private void OnSubmitChangesCommand(object obj, BaseNode node)
		{
			LaunchSubmitChangesForm();
		}

		public override void OnContextHelp()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).OnContextHelp();
			string strTopic = GlobalControl.HelpFile + "#html/Properties_and_Parameters___Pure_Sheet.htm";
			ApedApplication.APEDHtmlHelpDisplay(strTopic);
		}

		}

		public override void OnExpand()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).OnExpand();
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			mybExpand = true;
			try
			{
				updateChildNodes();
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing the list of databases: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				ErrorNode errorNode2 = new ErrorNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin);
				((BaseNode)errorNode2).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl propertiesAndParametersTabCtl = (ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				ShowPropertiesAndParameters(propertiesAndParametersTabCtl);
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing Compound Table: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00aa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ad: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00c2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c4: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl propertiesAndParametersTabCtl = (ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					ShowPropertiesAndParameters(propertiesAndParametersTabCtl);
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 11 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnDatabaseUpdated(object sender, string databaseName)
		{
			if (databaseName == myDatabase.Alias && mybExpand)
			{
				((BaseNode)this).Remove(true);
				updateChildNodes();
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl propertiesAndParametersTabCtl = (ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowPropertiesAndParameters(propertiesAndParametersTabCtl);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowPropertiesAndParameters(ApedPropertiesAndParametersTabCtl propertiesAndParametersTabCtl)
		{
			if (propertiesAndParametersTabCtl != null)
			{
				if (myApedDatabase.bProperties)
				{
					ApedUtils.GetAllProperties(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				propertiesAndParametersTabCtl.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				propertiesAndParametersTabCtl.SetTabPages(myApedDatabase.bUnaryProperties, myApedDatabase.bBinaryParameters, myApedDatabase.bTernaryParameters, myApedDatabase.bPairParameters);
				((Control)(object)propertiesAndParametersTabCtl).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void updateChildNodes()
		{
			if (myApedDatabase.bProperties)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllProperties(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bUnaryProperties)
			{
				bPure = true;
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myPureNode = new PureNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myPureNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bBinaryParameters)
			{
				bBinary = true;
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myBinaryNode = new BinaryNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myBinaryNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bTernaryParameters)
			{
				bTernary = true;
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myTernaryNode = new TernaryNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myTernaryNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
			}
			if (myApedDatabase.bPairParameters)
			{
				bPair = true;
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myPairNode = new PairNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myPairNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
			}
			if (myDatabase.DatabaseVersion != 2006)
			{
				bReactions = true;
				if (myApedDatabase.bView)
				{
					myReactionsNode = new ReactionsNode(((BaseNode)this).Snapin, myDatabase, myRootCompounds, myApedDatabase, myDataManager);
					((BaseNode)myReactionsNode).Insert((BaseNode)(object)this);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void LaunchPropertiesAndParametersManagerDlg()
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedPropertiesAndParametersManagerForm val = new ApedPropertiesAndParametersManagerForm();
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.apedPropertiesAndParametersManagerTabCtl1.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
		}

		internal void LaunchViewChangesForm()
		{
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.AllChangedProperties != null && ((SortedList)myApedDatabase.AllChangedProperties).Count > 0)
			{
				ApedViewChangesForm val = new ApedViewChangesForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			else if (myApedDatabase.AllChangedReactions != null && ((SortedList)myApedDatabase.AllChangedReactions).Count > 0)
			{
				ApedViewChangesForm val2 = new ApedViewChangesForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				((Form)(object)val2).ShowDialog();
			}
			else
			{
				MessageBox.Show("Currently there are no pending changes", "Clear Changes");
			}
		}

		internal void LaunchSubmitChangesForm()
		{
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0037: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0076: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.AllChangedProperties != null && ((SortedList)myApedDatabase.AllChangedProperties).Count > 0)
			{
				ApedSubmitForm val = new ApedSubmitForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				((Form)(object)val).ShowDialog();
			}
			else if (myApedDatabase.AllChangedReactions != null && ((SortedList)myApedDatabase.AllChangedReactions).Count > 0)
			{
				ApedSubmitForm val2 = new ApedSubmitForm(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				((Form)(object)val2).ShowDialog();
			}
			else
			{
				MessageBox.Show("Currently there are no pending changes", "Clear Changes");
			}
		}
	}
	internal class PureDataNode : FormNode, IFindCompoundLauncher
	{
		private IDatabase myDatabase;

		private IDatabank myDatabank;

		private IDatabankCollection myDatabanks;

		private DbankCompoundList myRootCompounds;

		private DbankCompoundList mySelectedCompounds;

		private ICompoundPropertyGateway myCompGateway;

		private IApedDatabase myApedDatabase;

		private IApedDatabank myApedDatabank;

		private bool mybShow;

		private ApedNodeType myNodeType = (ApedNodeType)13;

		private string myNodeName = "";

		private IApedMMC myDataManager;

		public bool bShow
		{
			get
			{
				return mybShow;
			}
			set
			{
				mybShow = value;
			}
		}

		public IDatabase Database => myDatabase;

		public PureDataNode(SnapinBase snapin, IDatabank databank, DbankCompoundList rootCompounds, DbankCompoundList selectedCompounds, IApedDatabase apedDatabase, IApedDatabank apedDatabank, IApedMMC dataManager)
			: base(snapin)
		{
			//IL_0003: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0082: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0092: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			myDatabank = databank;
			myDatabase = myDatabank.Database;
			myApedDatabase = apedDatabase;
			myApedDatabank = apedDatabank;
			myNodeName = myApedDatabase.Name + myDatabank.Name + "Pure";
			myDataManager = dataManager;
			myDatabanks = (IDatabankCollection)apedDatabase.Databanks;
			myRootCompounds = rootCompounds;
			mySelectedCompounds = selectedCompounds;
			myCompGateway = (ICompoundPropertyGateway)new CompoundPropertyGateway();
			myCompGateway.Databanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			Initialize();
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			((BaseNode)this).DisplayName = "Pure";
			SetImageIndicesForNode();
			AddHandlers();
			AddMenuItems();

		private bool DBContainCompound(IDbankCompound dbc, ArrayList dbankcompIDs)
		{
			foreach (int dbankcompID in dbankcompIDs)
			{
				string text = dbankcompID.ToString();
				if (text == dbc.CompoundObjectOfId.CompoundIndex)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		{
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnDeselectScope();

			{
				ApedPureDataTabCtl val = (ApedPureDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;

					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a0: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ShowCompounds();
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if ((int)updateType == 21)
			{
				_ = database == myApedDatabase.Name;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0075: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0077: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0082: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedPureDataTabCtl val = (ApedPureDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowAllCompounds(val);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedPureDataTabCtl val = (ApedPureDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				if (val.Databank == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabank(myDatabank);
				}
				if (val.CompoundPropertyGateway == null)
				{
					val.SetGateway(myCompGateway);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedPureDataTabCtl pureDataTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				pureDataTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				pureDataTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		}

		}

		public void GetSelectedDatabanks(out IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabank != null)
			{
				selectedDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			}
			else
			{
				selectedDbanks = null;
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedDatabanks(IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
		}

			availableDbanks = myDatabanks;
		}

		public void SetAvailableDatabanks(IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
		}

		{
			return true;
		}

		{
			return "water";
		}

					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedPureTabCtl val = (ApedPureTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 16 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedPureTabCtl val = (ApedPureTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if ((int)updateType == 2)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					else
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedPureTabCtl val = (ApedPureTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedPureTabCtl pureTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0046: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myApedDatabase.bDatabanks)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllDatabanks(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					arrayList.Add(val.Compound);
				}
				pureTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				pureTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		{
			return false;
		}

		public bool CanShowDatabanksTab()
		{
			return false;
		}

		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabank != null)
			{
				selectedDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			}
			else
			{
				selectedDbanks = null;
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedDatabanks(IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
		}

		public void GetAvailableDatabanks(out IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			IDatabank databank = myApedDatabase.Databank;

		}

		}

		}

		protected internal void LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg()

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowCompounds();
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing reactions data " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedReactionsDataTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 25 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedReactionsDataTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if ((int)updateType == 2)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					else
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedReactionsDataTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsDataTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase, myDatabank);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedReactionsDataTabCtl reactionsDataTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0080: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a1: Expected O, but got Unknown
			_ = myApedDatabase.bDatabanks;
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					CompoundObject value = new CompoundObject(val.Compound.Id, val.Compound.Compound1Name, val.AspenAlias, val.Compound.Compound3Name, val.Compound.Compound4Name, val.Compound.Identifier, val.Compound.CompoundType, val.Compound.InteractionType, val.Compound.IsReverse);
					arrayList.Add(value);
				}
				reactionsDataTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
			}
			else
			{
				reactionsDataTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
		}

		protected internal void LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			DbankCompoundDialogBox val = new DbankCompoundDialogBox((IFindCompoundLauncher)(object)this);
			if ((int)val.TabDialogControl.OKorCancel == 1)
			{
				if (val != null)
				{
					val = null;
				}
			}
			else if (val != null)
			{
				val = null;
			}
		}

		public override SCOPEDATAITEM GetScopeDataItem(BaseNode parentNode)

			scopeDataItem.cChildren = 0;

		}

		public override void OnDeselectScope()

				ApedReactionsTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowCompounds();
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing reactions data " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00b3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b6: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00cb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cd: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
				{
					return;
				}
				try
				{
					ApedReactionsTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
					if (val != null)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (GatewayException ex)
				{
					if (ex.Message == "")
					{
						string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
						if (text != "")
						{
							MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception ex2)
				{
					if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (((int)updateType != 24 || !(database == myApedDatabase.Name)) && (int)updateType == 4 && database == myApedDatabase.Name)
			{
				myDatabase = myApedDatabase.Database;
			}
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedReactionsTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if (val != null)
				{
					if ((int)updateType == 2)
					{
						ShowAllCompounds(val);
						val.RefreshAll();
					}
					else
					{
						_ = 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ApedReactionsTabCtl val = (ApedReactionsTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.NewCompoundsAdded += new ApedSelectedCompoundsCtlEventHandler(OnCompoundsAdded);
				if (val.Database == null)
				{
					val.SetDatabase(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
				}
				ShowAllCompounds(val);
				((Control)(object)val).Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void ShowAllCompounds(ApedReactionsTabCtl reactionsTabCtl)
		{
			//IL_0049: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0092: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ad: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			if (myApedDatabase.bDatabanks)
			{
				ApedUtils.GetAllDatabanks(myDatabase, myApedDatabase);
			}
			if (myRootCompounds.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (IDbankCompound item in (IEnumerable)myRootCompounds)
				{
					IDbankCompound val = item;
					CompoundObject value = new CompoundObject(val.Compound.Id, val.Compound.Compound1Name, val.AspenAlias, val.Compound.Compound3Name, val.Compound.Compound4Name, val.Compound.Identifier, val.Compound.CompoundType, val.Compound.InteractionType, val.Compound.IsReverse);
					arrayList.Add(value);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				reactionsTabCtl.ActivateAddCompounds();
			}
			reactionsTabCtl.SetWorkingCompounds((ICollection)arrayList);
		}

		public void CompoundsAdded(IList AddedCompounds)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ArrayList dbankCompounds = (ArrayList)AddedCompounds;
			myRootCompounds.Adds(dbankCompounds, myNodeType, myNodeName);
		}

		public bool CanModifyDatabankLists()
		{
			return false;
		}

		public bool CanShowDatabanksTab()
		{
			return false;
		}

		public void GetSelectedDatabanks(out IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (myDatabank != null)
			{
				selectedDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { myDatabank });
			}
			else
			{
				selectedDbanks = null;
			}
		}

		public void SetSelectedDatabanks(IDatabankCollection selectedDbanks)
		{
		}

		public void GetAvailableDatabanks(out IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IDatabank databank = myApedDatabase.Databank;
			availableDbanks = (IDatabankCollection)new DatabankCollection((IDatabank[])(object)new IDatabank[1] { databank });
		}

		public void SetAvailableDatabanks(IDatabankCollection availableDbanks)
		{
		}

		public bool FilterEnabled()
		{
			return true;
		}

		public string GetInitialSearchString()

		}

			SqlServer,
			Oracle
		}

		private Button btnCancel;

		private Container components;

		private IDatabase myDatabase;

		private SortedList myApedDatabases;

		private bool mybEdit;

		public bool bEdit
		{
			get
			{
				return mybEdit;
			}
			set
			{
				mybEdit = value;
				if (mybEdit)
				{
					registeredDatabaseControl1.bEdit = true;
					registeredDatabaseControl1.SetEditStatus();
				}
			}
		}

		{

			}
		}

		public DatabaseTypes DatabaseType
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if ((int)registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType == 0)
				{
					return DatabaseTypes.SqlServer;
				}
				return DatabaseTypes.Oracle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == DatabaseTypes.SqlServer)
				{
					registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType = (DatabaseTypes)0;
				}
				else
				{
					registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType = (DatabaseTypes)1;
				}
			}
		}

			}
		}

		{
			InitializeComponent();
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			myDatabase = database;
			btnOkay.Visible = false;
			btnCancel.Text = "Close";
			registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType = (DatabaseTypes)0;
			registeredDatabaseControl1.SetApedDatabases(myApedDatabases, myDatabase);
		}

		public RegisteredDatabaseForm(SortedList apedDatabases, string lastServerName, string lastUserName)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			myApedDatabases = apedDatabases;
			GlobalControl.bRegister = false;
			registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType = (DatabaseTypes)0;
			registeredDatabaseControl1.SetApedDatabases(myApedDatabases);
			if (!lastServerName.Contains("pipe") && !lastServerName.Contains("tsql") && !lastServerName.Contains("query") && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(lastServerName))
			{
				registeredDatabaseControl1.SetLastServerAndUserName(lastServerName, lastUserName);
			}
		}

		public RegisteredDatabaseForm()

			registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseType = (DatabaseTypes)0;
		}

		{

		private void OnKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Tab || e.KeyCode == Keys.Return)
			{
				registeredDatabaseControl1.SetFocus();
			}
		}

		private void btnOkay_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApedGlobal.bCheck = true;
		}

		private void btnOkay_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (registeredDatabaseControl1.ValidateInput())
			{
				GlobalControl.bRegister = true;
				base.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
			}
		}

		private void btnCancel_MouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApedGlobal.bCheck = false;
		}

		private void btnCancel_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			GlobalControl.bRegister = false;
			base.DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel;
			Close();
		}

		private bool validateInput()
		{
			if (registeredDatabaseControl1.DatabaseAlias == null)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("The database alias is required.", null, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Asterisk);
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void btnOkay_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			sourceDatabase = cmbSource.Text;
			targetDatabase = cmbTarget.Text;
			base.DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
		}

		private void radioSyncCopyTo_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			CheckComboBox();
			syncType = 1;
		}

		private void radioSyncCopyChangesTo_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			CheckComboBox();
			syncType = 2;
		}

		private void radioSyncCopyCreateNew_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			CheckComboBox();
			syncType = 3;
		}

		private void AddDatabasesToComboBoxes()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < myDatabases.Count; i++)
			{
				cmbSource.Items.Add(myDatabases[i]);
				cmbTarget.Items.Add(myDatabases[i]);
			}
			if (cmbSource.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				cmbSource.SelectedIndex = 0;
			}
			if (cmbTarget.Items.Count > 1)
			{
				cmbTarget.SelectedIndex = 1;
			}
		}

		private void CheckComboBox()
		{
			if (radioSyncCopyCreateNew.Checked)
			{
				cmbTarget.Enabled = false;
				btnAddTargetDatabase.Enabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				cmbTarget.Enabled = true;
				btnAddTargetDatabase.Enabled = true;
			}
		}

		private void btnAddTargetDatabase_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			AddDatabase(IsSource: false);
		}

		private void AddDatabase(bool IsSource)
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			try
			{
				RegisteredDatabaseForm registeredDatabaseForm = new RegisteredDatabaseForm();
				if (registeredDatabaseForm.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
				{
					string item = "";
					string text = null;
					string text2 = null;
					if (registeredDatabaseForm.Username != "")
					{
						text = registeredDatabaseForm.Username;
						text2 = registeredDatabaseForm.Password;
					}
					if (registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseType == RegisteredDatabaseForm.DatabaseTypes.SqlServer)
					{
						SqlServerDatabase val = new SqlServerDatabase();
						val.Connect(registeredDatabaseForm.DatabaseAlias[0].ToString(), registeredDatabaseForm.ServerName, text, text2, registeredDatabaseForm.InitialCatalog[0].ToString());
						addedDatabase = (IDatabase)(object)val;
						item = ((Database)val).Alias;
					}
					if (IsSource)
					{
						cmbSource.Items.Add(item);
						cmbSource.SelectedIndex = cmbSource.Items.Count - 1;
					}
					else
					{
						cmbTarget.Items.Add(item);
						cmbTarget.SelectedIndex = cmbTarget.Items.Count - 1;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("There was an error adding the database. " + ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
			}
		}

		private void btnAddSourceDatabase_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			AddDatabase(IsSource: true);
		}
	}

		private void AddHandlers()
		{
			//IL_0008: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_004d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0057: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0064: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((BaseNode)this).OnToolbarClickEvent += new NodeNotificationHandler(onToolbarClickEvent);
			myRootCompounds.CompoundsAdded += AddCompoundsToSelectedList;
			myRootCompounds.CompoundsRemoved += RemoveCompoundsFromSelectedList;
			myApedDatabase.DatabaseUpdate += new ApedDatabaseEventHandler(OnUpdateDatabase);
			myDataManager.DataManagerUpdate += new ApedMMCEventHandler(OnDataManagerUpdate);
		}

		private void OnDataManagerUpdate(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType, string database)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0016: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)updateType == 1)
			{
				_ = ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName;
			}
			else if ((int)updateType == 18)
			{
				_ = database == myApedDatabase.Name;
			}
		}

		private void onToolbarClickEvent(object sender, NodeEventArgs args)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			int command = ((CommandArgs)args).m_command;
			if (command != 4)
			{
				_ = 5;
			}
		}

		}

		private void AddCompoundsToSelectedList(DbankCompoundList sender, ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)

		}

		private void RemoveCompoundsFromSelectedList(DbankCompoundList sender, ArrayList compounds, ApedNodeType nodeType, string nodeName)

			}
		}

		protected internal void LaunchSelectCompoundsDlg()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			{
				val = null;
			}
		}

		public override bool OnInitOcx(object objOCX)
		{
			((FormNode)this).OnInitOcx(objOCX);
			((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
			ApedGlobal.NodeName = myNodeName;
			try
			{
				ShowCompounds();
				return true;
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MessageBox.Show("An error occurred initializing ternary properties: " + ex2.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			finally
			{
				((Control)(object)((BaseNode)this).Snapin).Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnUpdateDatabase(object sender, ApedUpdateType updateType)
		{
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0021: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_002e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (!(ApedGlobal.NodeName == myNodeName))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				ApedTernaryTabCtl ternaryTabCtl = (ApedTernaryTabCtl)((FormNode)this).UserControl;
				if ((int)updateType == 2)
				{
					ShowAllCompounds(ternaryTabCtl);
				}
				else
				{
					_ = 1;
				}
			}
			catch (GatewayException ex)
			{
				if (ex.Message == "")
				{
					string text = ApedGlobal.GatewayError();
					if (text != "")
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex.Message, "APED", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				if (!ex2.Message.Contains("Cannot access a disposed object"))
				{
					MessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, "Aped", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ShowCompounds()

		}
